Method: 提出三方面改进：1) 硬件表征（力校准、延迟和超调测量）；2) 基于MuJoCo的sim2real验证模型，用于解析宽度到抓取规划；3) 混合闭环速度-力抓取控制器。这些模块化组件可与外部物体检测器和视觉语言模型兼容。

Result: 在孔轴装配任务中达到65%成功率，显著优于仅使用腕力的基线（10%）。在15种物理特性不同的物体上进行300次抓取测试，达到87%成功率，优于无规划抓取和学习式抓取方法。

Conclusion: 通过系统化改进将Inspire RH56DFX从黑盒设备转变为可解释、可立即部署的研究工具，提供了模块化、兼容外部感知系统的灵巧操作接口，相关成果已开源。

Abstract: Commercially accessible dexterous robot hands are increasingly prevalent, but many remain difficult to use as scientific instruments. For example, the Inspire RH56DFX hand exposes only uncalibrated proprioceptive information and shows unreliable contact behavior at high speed (up to 1618% force limit overshoot). Furthermore, its underactuated, coupled finger linkages make antipodal grasps non-trivial. We contribute three improvements to the Inspire RH56DFX to transform it from a black-box device to a research tool: (1) hardware characterization (force calibration, latency, and overshoot), (2) a sim2real validated MuJoCo model for analytical width-to-grasp planning, and (3) a hybrid, closed-loop speed-force grasp controller. We validate these components on peg-in-hole insertion, achieving 65% success and outperforming a wrist-force-only baseline of 10% and on 300 grasps across 15 physically diverse objects, achieving 87% success and outperforming plan-free grasps and learned grasps. Our approach is modular, designed for compatibility with external object detectors and vision-language models for width & force estimation and high-level planning, and provides an interpretable and immediately deployable interface for dexterous manipulation with the Inspire RH56DFX hand, open-sourced at this website https://correlllab.github.io/rh56dfx.html.

</details>


### [60] [Improving through Interaction: Searching Behavioral Representation Spaces with CMA-ES-IG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09011)
*Nathaniel Dennler,Zhonghao Shi,Yiran Tao,Andreea Bobu,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMA-ES-IG算法，通过生成感知上显著且信息丰富的轨迹供用户排序，将用户体验考虑纳入机器人偏好学习过程，在保持计算可行性的同时提高高维空间的可扩展性和对噪声反馈的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人偏好学习技术主要关注优化学习结果（如样本效率或最终偏好估计精度），但忽视了用户在提供排序过程中的关键期望，这可能负面影响用户对机器人系统的采用。需要将用户体验考虑纳入偏好学习过程。

Method: 提出Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategies with Information Gain (CMA-ES-IG)算法，该算法通过生成感知上显著且信息丰富的轨迹供用户排序，在偏好学习过程中显式地融入用户体验考虑。

Result: 通过仿真研究和真实机器人实验证明，与现有最优方法相比，CMA-ES-IG：(1)能更有效地扩展到更高维偏好空间；(2)对高维问题保持计算可行性；(3)对噪声或不一致的用户反馈具有鲁棒性；(4)非专家用户更倾向于使用它来识别其偏好的机器人行为。

Conclusion: CMA-ES-IG算法通过将用户体验考虑纳入偏好学习过程，在保持计算效率的同时提高了在高维空间的可扩展性和对噪声反馈的鲁棒性，为非专家用户提供了更好的机器人行为偏好识别体验。

Abstract: Robots that interact with humans must adapt to individual users' preferences to operate effectively in human-centered environments. An intuitive and effective technique to learn non-expert users' preferences is through rankings of robot behaviors, e.g., trajectories, gestures, or voices. Existing techniques primarily focus on generating queries that optimize preference learning outcomes, such as sample efficiency or final preference estimation accuracy. However, the focus on outcome overlooks key user expectations in the process of providing these rankings, which can negatively impact users' adoption of robotic systems. This work proposes the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategies with Information Gain (CMA-ES-IG) algorithm. CMA-ES-IG explicitly incorporates user experience considerations into the preference learning process by suggesting perceptually distinct and informative trajectories for users to rank. We demonstrate these benefits through both simulated studies and real-robot experiments. CMA-ES-IG, compared to state-of-the-art alternatives, (1) scales more effectively to higher-dimensional preference spaces, (2) maintains computational tractability for high-dimensional problems, (3) is robust to noisy or inconsistent user feedback, and (4) is preferred by non-expert users in identifying their preferred robot behaviors. This project's code is available at github.com/interaction-lab/CMA-ES-IG

</details>


### [61] [PlayWorld: Learning Robot World Models from Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09030)
*Tenny Yin,Zhiting Mei,Zhonghe Zheng,Miyu Yamane,David Wang,Jade Sceats,Samuel M. Bateman,Lihan Zha,Apurva Badithela,Ola Shorinwa,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlayWorld：通过无监督机器人自玩训练高保真视频世界模拟器的自主管道，相比人类收集数据能生成更物理一致的交互预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大规模机器人数据集训练的视频模型在预测物理一致的机器人-物体交互方面仍有困难，这些交互对机器人操作至关重要。现有方法依赖有偏的人类演示数据，无法捕捉复杂的长尾物理交互

Method: 提出PlayWorld，一个简单、可扩展、完全自主的管道，通过无监督机器人自玩来训练高保真视频世界模拟器。系统从无监督的机器人自我游戏中学习，实现自然可扩展的数据收集，同时捕捉复杂的长尾物理交互

Result: 在多样化操作任务中，PlayWorld生成了高质量、物理一致的接触丰富交互预测，这些是人类收集数据训练的世界模型无法捕捉的。在细粒度失败预测和策略评估方面表现优异，相比人类收集数据提升达40%。在真实世界部署中，通过世界模型中的强化学习将策略成功率提升了65%

Conclusion: PlayWorld通过无监督机器人自玩训练视频世界模拟器，解决了现有方法在物理一致性预测方面的不足，为构建通用机器人模拟器提供了更有效的数据收集和训练方法，显著提升了模拟质量和下游应用性能

Abstract: Action-conditioned video models offer a promising path to building general-purpose robot simulators that can improve directly from data. Yet, despite training on large-scale robot datasets, current state-of-the-art video models still struggle to predict physically consistent robot-object interactions that are crucial in robotic manipulation. To close this gap, we present PlayWorld, a simple, scalable, and fully autonomous pipeline for training high-fidelity video world simulators from interaction experience. In contrast to prior approaches that rely on success-biased human demonstrations, PlayWorld is the first system capable of learning entirely from unsupervised robot self-play, enabling naturally scalable data collection while capturing complex, long-tailed physical interactions essential for modeling realistic object dynamics. Experiments across diverse manipulation tasks show that PlayWorld generates high-quality, physically consistent predictions for contact-rich interactions that are not captured by world models trained on human-collected data.We further demonstrate the versatility of PlayWorld in enabling fine-grained failure prediction and policy evaluation, with up to 40% improvements over human-collected data. Finally, we demonstrate how PlayWorld enables reinforcement learning in the world model, improving policy performance by 65% in success rates when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [62] [ImpedanceDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Global Path Planning for UAV Swarm Navigation with Generative Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09031)
*Faryal Batool,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ImpedanceDiffusion：用于无人机群在杂乱室内环境导航的分层框架，结合图像条件扩散全局路径规划、APF跟踪和语义感知可变阻抗控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱室内环境中的安全群导航需要长时域规划、反应式避障和自适应顺应性，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求

Method: 分层框架：扩散模型从RGB图像生成全局轨迹，APF层进行反应式跟踪，VLM-RAG模块进行语义障碍物分类以自适应调整阻抗参数

Result: 两种扩散规划器均实现100%轨迹生成率，92%成功率，无飞行碰撞，保持稳定阻抗编队控制，不同规划器产生不同速度特性

Conclusion: ImpedanceDiffusion框架在杂乱室内环境中实现了可靠自适应的群导航，结合了长时域规划、反应式避障和语义感知顺应性控制

Abstract: Safe swarm navigation in cluttered indoor environment requires long-horizon planning, reactive obstacle avoidance, and adaptive compliance. We propose ImpedanceDiffusion, a hierarchical framework that leverages image-conditioned diffusion-based global path planning with Artificial Potential Field (APF) tracking and semantic-aware variable impedance control for aerial drone swarms.
  The diffusion model generates geometric global trajectories directly from RGB images without explicit map construction. These trajectories are tracked by an APF-based reactive layer, while a VLM-RAG module performs semantic obstacle classification with 90% retrieval accuracy to adapt impedance parameters for mixed obstacle environments during execution.
  Two diffusion planners are evaluated: (i) a top-view long-horizon planner using single-pass inference and (ii) a first-person-view (FPV) short-horizon planner deployed via a two-stage inference pipeline. Both planners achieve a 100% trajectory generation rate across twenty static and dynamic experimental configurations and are validated via zero-shot sim-to-real deployment on Crazyflie 2.1 drones through the hierarchical APF-impedance control stack. The top-view planner produces smoother trajectories that yield conservative tracking speeds of 1.0-1.2 m/s near hard obstacles and 0.6-1.0 m/s near soft obstacles. In contrast, the FPV planner generates trajectories with greater local clearance and typically higher speeds, reaching 1.4-2.0 m/s near hard obstacles and up to 1.6 m/s near soft obstacles. Across 20 experimental configurations (100 total runs), the framework achieved a 92% success rate while maintaining stable impedance-based formation control with bounded oscillations and no in-flight collisions, demonstrating reliable and adaptive swarm navigation in cluttered indoor environments.

</details>


### [63] [Cutting the Cord: System Architecture for Low-Cost, GPU-Accelerated Bimanual Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09051)
*Artemis Shaw,Chen Liu,Justin Costa,Rane Gray,Alina Skowronek,Kevin Diaz,Nam Bui,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于开源XLeRobot平台构建的双臂移动机械臂，成本低于1300美元，具备板载计算能力，支持远程操作、自主SLAM导航和视觉操控


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器人研究和教育提供低成本、功能完整的移动操作平台，减少对外部依赖，实现自主操作

Method: 1. 优化机械设计最大化刚度重量比；2. 采用Tri-Bus电源拓扑隔离计算与电机引起的电压瞬变；3. 集成NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano实现嵌入式自主操作

Result: 开发出成本低于1300美元的双臂移动机械臂平台，支持远程操作、自主SLAM导航和基于视觉的操控，无需外部依赖

Conclusion: 该平台为机器人和机器人学习领域的研究和教育提供了经济实用的替代方案，具备完整的自主操作能力

Abstract: We present a bimanual mobile manipulator built on the open-source XLeRobot with integrated onboard compute for less than \$1300. Key contributions include: (1) optimized mechanical design maximizing stiffness-to-weight ratio, (2) a Tri-Bus power topology isolating compute from motor-induced voltage transients, and (3) embedded autonomy using NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano for untethered operation. The platform enables teleoperation, autonomous SLAM navigation, and vision-based manipulation without external dependencies, providing a low-cost alternative for research and education in robotics and robot learning.

</details>


### [64] [Quality over Quantity: Demonstration Curation via Influence Functions for Data-Centric Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09056)
*Haeone Lee,Taywon Min,Junsu Kim,Sinjae Kang,Fangchen Liu,Lerrel Pinto,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QoQ：基于影响力函数的数据质量评估方法，通过量化训练样本对验证集损失的贡献来筛选高质量机器人演示数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于演示的机器人学习面临数据质量瓶颈：人类遥操作引入的错误、操作约束和变异性导致噪声和次优行为，而现有数据筛选方法多为手动启发式，缺乏系统性

Method: 提出QoQ方法，将数据质量定义为训练样本对验证演示损失的贡献度，使用影响力函数量化个体训练样本对模型性能的影响，并引入两项关键技术：1）使用验证样本中的最大影响力捕捉最相关的状态-动作对；2）聚合同一轨迹内状态-动作对的影响力分数以减少噪声并提高数据覆盖率

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，QoQ方法相比现有数据选择方法能持续提升策略性能

Conclusion: QoQ提供了一种基于理论的数据质量评估框架，能够系统性地识别高质量演示数据，有效解决机器人学习中的数据质量瓶颈问题

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations has emerged as a promising paradigm for end-to-end robot control, particularly when scaled to diverse and large datasets. However, the quality of demonstration data, often collected through human teleoperation, remains a critical bottleneck for effective data-driven robot learning. Human errors, operational constraints, and teleoperator variability introduce noise and suboptimal behaviors, making data curation essential yet largely manual and heuristic-driven. In this work, we propose Quality over Quantity (QoQ), a grounded and systematic approach to identifying high-quality data by defining data quality as the contribution of each training sample to reducing loss on validation demonstrations. To efficiently estimate this contribution, we leverage influence functions, which quantify the impact of individual training samples on model performance. We further introduce two key techniques to adapt influence functions for robot demonstrations: (i) using maximum influence across validation samples to capture the most relevant state-action pairs, and (ii) aggregating influence scores of state-action pairs within the same trajectory to reduce noise and improve data coverage. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that QoQ consistently improves policy performances over prior data selection methods.

</details>


### [65] [3D UAV Trajectory Estimation and Classification from Internet Videos via Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09070)
*Haoxiang Lei,Daotong Wang,Shenghai Yuan,Jianbo Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需人工标注、从互联网规模无人机视频中自动提取3D轨迹和类别信息的框架，通过语言驱动数据采集、跨模态标签生成和物理约束优化实现，在零样本迁移实验中展现数据规模效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反无人机系统需要可靠的3D轨迹估计，但大规模精确标注的轨迹数据获取成本过高。现有方法依赖人工标注，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 1) 语言驱动数据采集：自动发现和收集无人机相关视频，通过视觉-语言推理筛选任务相关片段；2) 免训练跨模态标签生成：推断3D轨迹假设和无人机类型线索；3) 物理信息细化：对估计轨迹施加时间平滑性和运动学一致性约束。

Result: 在公开的3D无人机基准测试上进行零样本迁移实验，结果显示随着在线视频数据量增加，目标数据集上的零样本迁移性能持续提升，无需目标域训练。该方法接近当前最先进水平。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够从互联网视频中自动生成无人机3D轨迹和类别标注，为下游反无人机任务提供可用数据。展示了数据规模效应和零样本迁移能力，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Reliable 3D trajectory estimation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is a fundamental requirement for anti-UAV systems, yet the acquisition of large-scale and accurately annotated trajectory data remains prohibitively expensive. In this work, we present a novel framework that derives UAV 3D trajectories and category information directly from Internet-scale UAV videos, without relying on manual annotations. First, language-driven data acquisition is employed to autonomously discover and collect UAV-related videos, while vision-language reasoning progressively filters task-relevant segments. Second, a training-free cross-modal label generation module is introduced to infer 3D trajectory hypotheses and UAV type cues. Third, a physics-informed refinement process is designed to impose temporal smoothness and kinematic consistency on the estimated trajectories. The resulting video clips and trajectory annotations can be readily utilized for downstream anti-UAV tasks. To assess effectiveness and generalization, we conduct zero-shot transfer experiments on a public, well-annotated 3D UAV benchmark. Results reveal a clear data scaling behavior: as the amount of online video data increases, zero-shot transfer performance on the target dataset improves consistently, without any target-domain training. The proposed method closely approaches the current state-of-the-art, highlighting its robustness and applicability to real-world anti-UAV scenarios. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [High-Slip-Ratio Control for Peak Tire-Road Friction Estimation Using Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09073)
*Zhaohui Liang,Hang Zhou,Heye Huanh,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种高滑移率控制框架，使自动驾驶车辆能够在空载运行时主动激发峰值摩擦区域，同时保持操作安全，用于准确估计轮胎-路面摩擦系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮胎-路面摩擦系数估计方法大多依赖常规车辆的自然驾驶数据，这些数据通常在温和加减速下采集，滑移激励不足，对峰值摩擦系数的可观测性有限，难以确保恶劣路况下的车辆安全控制。

Method: 采用简化的Magic Formula轮胎模型表示非线性滑移-力动力学，通过重复高滑移测量进行局部拟合；提出约束最优控制策略平衡滑移激励、轨迹跟踪和碰撞避免；引入基于分箱的统计投影方法在噪声和局部稀疏性下鲁棒估计峰值摩擦系数。

Result: 通过闭环仿真和实车实验验证了该框架的准确性、安全性和可行性，证明其适用于可扩展、经济高效的道路摩擦筛查。

Conclusion: 该高滑移率控制框架能够使自动驾驶车辆主动激发峰值摩擦区域，在保持操作安全的同时准确估计轮胎-路面摩擦系数，为道路安全监测提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of the tire-road friction coefficient (TRFC) is critical for ensuring safe vehicle control, especially under adverse road conditions. However, most existing methods rely on naturalistic driving data from regular vehicles, which typically operate under mild acceleration and braking. As a result, the data provide insufficient slip excitation and offer limited observability of the peak TRFC. This paper presents a high-slip-ratio control framework that enables automated vehicles (AVs) to actively excite the peak friction region during empty-haul operations while maintaining operational safety. A simplified Magic Formula tire model is adopted to represent nonlinear slip-force dynamics and is locally fitted using repeated high-slip measurements. To support safe execution in car-following scenarios, we formulate a constrained optimal control strategy that balances slip excitation, trajectory tracking, and collision avoidance. In parallel, a binning-based statistical projection method is introduced to robustly estimate peak TRFC under noise and local sparsity. The framework is validated through both closed-loop simulations and real-vehicle experiments, demonstrating its accuracy, safety, and feasibility for scalable, cost-effective roadway friction screening.

</details>


### [67] [Provably Safe Trajectory Generation for Manipulators Under Motion and Environmental Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09083)
*Fei Meng,Zijiang Yang,Xinyu Mao,Haobo Liang,Max Q. -H. Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度随机Koopman算子建模、分层验证和MPPI控制的风险有界运动规划框架，用于机器人操作臂在不确定非凸环境中的安全最优运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理复杂几何和非高斯不确定性时，难以提供高效且形式化认证的碰撞风险保证，特别是在不确定非凸环境中操作的机器人操作臂面临重大安全挑战。

Method: 1) 使用刚性操作臂深度随机Koopman算子(RM-DeSKO)模型预测机器人状态分布；2) 提出分层验证方法，结合可并行物理仿真和SOS规划作为细粒度形式化认证的过滤器；3) 将方法嵌入MPPI控制器，利用SOS分解的二进制碰撞信息改进策略。

Result: 在两个典型机器人操作臂上通过大量仿真和真实实验验证了框架有效性，包括具有挑战性的人机协作场景，展示了学习模型的仿真到真实迁移能力以及在复杂不确定环境中生成安全高效轨迹的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了机器人操作臂在不确定非凸环境中安全运动规划的挑战，通过集成学习建模、形式化验证和优化控制，实现了风险有界的高效运动规划，并展示了良好的仿真到真实迁移性能。

Abstract: Robot manipulators operating in uncertain and non-convex environments present significant challenges for safe and optimal motion planning. Existing methods often struggle to provide efficient and formally certified collision risk guarantees, particularly when dealing with complex geometries and non-Gaussian uncertainties. This article proposes a novel risk-bounded motion planning framework to address this unmet need. Our approach integrates a rigid manipulator deep stochastic Koopman operator (RM-DeSKO) model to robustly predict the robot's state distribution under motion uncertainty. We then introduce an efficient, hierarchical verification method that combines parallelizable physics simulations with sum-of-squares (SOS) programming as a filter for fine-grained, formal certification of collision risk. This method is embedded within a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller that uniquely utilizes binary collision information from SOS decomposition to improve its policy. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on two typical robot manipulators through extensive simulations and real-world experiments, including a challenging human-robot collaboration scenario, demonstrating sim-to-real transfer of the learned model and its ability to generate safe and efficient trajectories in complex, uncertain settings.

</details>


### [68] [Latent World Models for Automated Driving: A Unified Taxonomy, Evaluation Framework, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09086)
*Rongxiang Zeng,Yongqi Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的潜在空间框架，用于综合自动驾驶领域世界模型的最新进展，通过潜在表示形式和结构先验组织设计空间，并阐述五个跨领域内部机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型和视觉-语言-动作系统正在重塑自动驾驶，但需要统一的框架来组织设计空间、连接设计选择与系统性能，并减少开环/闭环评估的不匹配。

Method: 提出潜在空间框架，按潜在表示目标（潜在世界、潜在动作、潜在生成器）和形式（连续状态、离散标记、混合）组织设计空间；引入几何、拓扑和语义的结构先验；阐述五个内部机制：结构同构、长期时间稳定性、语义与推理对齐、价值对齐目标与后训练、自适应计算与深思熟虑。

Result: 建立了自动驾驶世界模型的分类框架，提出了具体评估方案（闭环指标套件和资源感知深思熟虑成本），识别了可操作的研究方向，旨在实现决策就绪、可验证且资源高效的自动驾驶系统。

Conclusion: 潜在空间框架为自动驾驶世界模型提供了统一的设计和评估基础，通过连接内部机制与系统性能，推动向决策就绪、可验证且资源高效的自动驾驶系统发展。

Abstract: Emerging generative world models and vision-language-action (VLA) systems are rapidly reshaping automated driving by enabling scalable simulation, long-horizon forecasting, and capability-rich decision making. Across these directions, latent representations serve as the central computational substrate: they compress high-dimensional multi-sensor observations, enable temporally coherent rollouts, and provide interfaces for planning, reasoning, and controllable generation. This paper proposes a unifying latent-space framework that synthesizes recent progress in world models for automated driving. The framework organizes the design space by the target and form of latent representations (latent worlds, latent actions, latent generators; continuous states, discrete tokens, and hybrids) and by structural priors for geometry, topology, and semantics. Building on this taxonomy, the paper articulates five cross-cutting internal mechanics (i.e, structural isomorphism, long-horizon temporal stability, semantic and reasoning alignment, value-aligned objectives and post-training, as well as adaptive computation and deliberation) and connects these design choices to robustness, generalization, and deployability. The work also proposes concrete evaluation prescriptions, including a closed-loop metric suite and a resource-aware deliberation cost, designed to reduce the open-loop / closed-loop mismatch. Finally, the paper identifies actionable research directions toward advancing latent world model for decision-ready, verifiable, and resource-efficient automated driving.

</details>


### [69] [PM-Nav: Priori-Map Guided Embodied Navigation in Functional Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09113)
*Jiang Gao,Xiangyu Dong,Haozhou Li,Haoran Zhao,Yaoming Zhou,Xiaoguang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PM-Nav：一种基于先验地图引导的具身导航框架，针对功能建筑中高度相似特征的导航挑战，通过语义先验地图、层次化思维链提示和多模型协作机制实现精确导航规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言驱动的具身导航范式在功能建筑中面临挑战，因为建筑内部特征高度相似，缺乏有效利用先验空间知识的能力，导致导航性能受限。

Method: 1. 将环境地图转换为导航友好的语义先验地图；2. 设计带有注释先验地图的层次化思维链提示模板，实现精确路径规划；3. 构建多模型协作动作输出机制，完成定位决策和执行控制。

Result: 在自制的功能建筑数据集上测试显示：在仿真环境中，PM-Nav相比SG-Nav和InstructNav分别提升511%和1175%；在真实世界中，分别提升650%和400%。

Conclusion: PM-Nav作为功能建筑的骨干导航框架具有巨大潜力，其显著性能提升证明了利用先验地图指导导航的有效性。

Abstract: Existing language-driven embodied navigation paradigms face challenges in functional buildings (FBs) with highly similar features, as they lack the ability to effectively utilize priori spatial knowledge. To tackle this issue, we propose a Priori-Map Guided Embodied Navigation (PM-Nav), wherein environmental maps are transformed into navigation-friendly semantic priori-maps, a hierarchical chain-of-thought prompt template with an annotation priori-map is designed to enable precise path planning, and a multi-model collaborative action output mechanism is built to accomplish positioning decisions and execution control for navigation planning. Comprehensive tests using a home-made FB dataset show that the PM-Nav obtains average improvements of 511\% and 1175\%, and 650\% and 400\% over the SG-Nav and the InstructNav in simulation and real-world, respectively. These tremendous boosts elucidate the great potential of using the PM-Nav as a backbone navigation framework for FBs.

</details>


### [70] [DexHiL: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for Vision-Language-Action Model Post-Training in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09121)
*Yifan Han,Zhongxi Chen,Yuxuan Zhao,Congsheng Xu,Yanming Shao,Yichuan Peng,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexHiL：首个面向灵巧VLA模型的集成式臂-手人机协同框架，通过干预感知数据采样和轻量遥操作界面提升灵巧操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中展现出良好泛化能力，但在特定复杂下游任务部署仍需有效后训练。现有灵巧VLA系统在多指控制的高维度、密集接触特性及与标准臂运动显著不同的执行分布下，可靠性和适应性受限，需要人机协同框架来提升性能

Method: 提出DexHiL框架：1) 干预感知数据采样策略，优先选择纠正段进行后训练；2) 轻量遥操作界面，支持执行期间即时人工校正；3) 集成式臂-手协同干预系统，在单一系统中实现臂和灵巧手的协调干预

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，DexHiL作为有效后训练框架，相比标准离线微调基线，在不同任务中平均成功率提升25%

Conclusion: DexHiL是首个面向灵巧VLA模型的集成式臂-手人机协同框架，通过协调干预和针对性数据采样，显著提升了灵巧操作的可靠性和适应性，为复杂机器人任务的后训练提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities in robotic manipulation, deploying them on specific and complex downstream tasks still demands effective post-training. In parallel, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) learning has proven to be a powerful mechanism for refining robot policies. However, extending this paradigm to dexterous manipulation remains challenging: multi-finger control is high-dimensional, contact-intensive, and exhibits execution distributions that differ markedly from standard arm motions, leaving existing dexterous VLA systems limited in reliability and adaptability. We present DexHiL, the first integrated arm-hand human-in-the-loop framework for dexterous VLA models, enabling coordinated interventions over the arm and the dexterous hand within a single system. DexHiL introduces an intervention-aware data sampling strategy that prioritizes corrective segments for post-training, alongside a lightweight teleoperation interface that supports instantaneous human corrections during execution. Real-robot experiments demonstrate that DexHiL serves as an effective post-training framework, yielding a substantial performance leap, outperforming standard offline-only fine-tuning baselines by an average of 25% in success rates across distinct tasks.
  Project page: https://chenzhongxi-sjtu.github.io/dexhil/

</details>


### [71] [SPAN-Nav: Generalized Spatial Awareness for Versatile Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09163)
*Jiahang Liu,Tianyu Xu,Jiawei Chen,Lu Yue,Jiazhao Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Minghan Li,Qisheng Zhao,Anqi Li,Qi Su,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPAN-Nav：通过单token空间先验和端到端框架，为视觉语言导航注入通用3D空间感知能力的基模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的具身导航方法在复杂环境中路径规划不可靠，主要原因是空间感知能力不足，需要增强对3D空间的通用理解

Method: 1) 通过大规模室内外环境的占据预测任务提取空间先验；2) 提出紧凑空间表示，发现单token足以封装导航所需粗粒度线索；3) 受思维链启发，通过端到端框架将单空间token显式注入动作推理；4) 多任务协同训练从广义空间先验中捕获任务自适应线索

Result: 1) 构建包含420万占据标注的大规模数据集；2) 在三个涵盖多样化场景和导航任务的基准测试中达到SOTA性能；3) 真实世界实验验证了在复杂物理场景中的鲁棒泛化能力和实际可靠性

Conclusion: SPAN-Nav通过单token空间表示和端到端框架成功为具身导航注入通用3D空间感知能力，实现了在多样化场景和任务中的鲁棒泛化，为可靠路径规划提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Recent embodied navigation approaches leveraging Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong generalization in versatile Vision-Language Navigation (VLN). However, reliable path planning in complex environments remains challenging due to insufficient spatial awareness. In this work, we introduce SPAN-Nav, an end-to-end foundation model designed to infuse embodied navigation with universal 3D spatial awareness using RGB video streams. SPAN-Nav extracts spatial priors across diverse scenes through an occupancy prediction task on extensive indoor and outdoor environments. To mitigate the computational burden, we introduce a compact representation for spatial priors, finding that a single token is sufficient to encapsulate the coarse-grained cues essential for navigation tasks. Furthermore, inspired by the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) mechanism, SPAN-Nav utilizes this single spatial token to explicitly inject spatial cues into action reasoning through an end-to end framework. Leveraging multi-task co-training, SPAN-Nav captures task-adaptive cues from generalized spatial priors, enabling robust spatial awareness to generalize even to the task lacking explicit spatial supervision. To support comprehensive spatial learning, we present a massive dataset of 4.2 million occupancy annotations that covers both indoor and outdoor scenes across multi-type navigation tasks. SPAN-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks spanning diverse scenarios and varied navigation tasks. Finally, real-world experiments validate the robust generalization and practical reliability of our approach across complex physical scenarios.

</details>


### [72] [ZeroWBC: Learning Natural Visuomotor Humanoid Control Directly from Human Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09170)
*Haoran Yang,Jiacheng Bao,Yucheng Xin,Haoming Song,Yuyang Tian,Bin Zhao,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroWBC：直接从人类第一人称视频学习自然人形机器人全身控制，无需大规模机器人遥操作数据，实现自然场景交互


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人场景交互控制存在局限性：1）受限于刚性运动模式，缺乏坐、踢等类人自然行为；2）依赖昂贵的遥操作数据采集，成本高、耗时长。需要一种更通用、自然且数据高效的控制方法。

Method: 1）微调视觉语言模型（VLM），基于文本指令和第一人称视觉上下文预测未来全身人体运动；2）将生成的运动重定向到真实机器人关节；3）通过鲁棒的通用运动跟踪策略执行人形机器人全身控制。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的大量实验表明，该方法在运动自然性和通用性方面优于基线方法，成功建立了消除全身控制遥操作数据采集开销的流程。

Conclusion: ZeroWBC提供了一种可扩展且高效的人形机器人全身控制范式，直接从人类视频学习自然运动，无需昂贵遥操作数据，实现了更自然、通用的人机场景交互。

Abstract: Achieving versatile and naturalistic whole-body control for humanoid robot scene-interaction remains a significant challenge. While some recent works have demonstrated autonomous humanoid interactive control, they are constrained to rigid locomotion patterns and expensive teleoperation data collection, lacking the versatility to execute more human-like natural behaviors such as sitting or kicking. Furthermore, acquiring the necessary real robot teleoperation data is prohibitively expensive and time-consuming. To address these limitations, we introduce ZeroWBC, a novel framework that learns a natural humanoid visuomotor control policy directly from human egocentric videos, eliminating the need for large-scale robot teleoperation data and enabling natural humanoid robot scene-interaction control. Specifically, our approach first fine-tunes a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to predict future whole-body human motions based on text instructions and egocentric visual context, then these generated motions are retargeted to real robot joints and executed via our robust general motion tracking policy for humanoid whole-body control. Extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot demonstrate that our method outperforms baseline approaches in motion naturalness and versatility, successfully establishing a pipeline that eliminates teleoperation data collection overhead for whole-body humanoid control, offering a scalable and efficient paradigm for general humanoid whole-body control.

</details>


### [73] [STONE Dataset: A Scalable Multi-Modal Surround-View 3D Traversability Dataset for Off-Road Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09175)
*Konyul Park,Daehun Kim,Jiyong Oh,Seunghoon Yu,Junseo Park,Jaehyun Park,Hongjae Shin,Hyungchan Cho,Jungho Kim,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STONE是一个用于越野导航的大规模多模态数据集，提供轨迹引导的3D可通行性地图和全面的环绕视图感知数据，包括128通道LiDAR、6个RGB相机和3个4D成像雷达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏可扩展性和多模态特性，限制了3D可通行性预测的进展。越野导航需要准确估计可通行区域并在多样化地形和感知条件下实现鲁棒感知。

Method: 开发了完全自动化的无标注流水线生成轨迹引导的3D可通行性地图：从LiDAR扫描重建密集地形表面，提取坡度、高程、粗糙度等几何属性，使用基于马氏距离的准则为机器人轨迹外的区域分配可通行性标签。

Result: 创建了STONE数据集，涵盖草地、农田、建筑工地、湖泊等多种环境和昼夜条件，包含同步的128通道LiDAR、6个RGB相机和3个4D成像雷达数据。建立了体素级3D可通行性预测基准，并在单模态和多模态设置下提供了强基线模型。

Conclusion: STONE通过自动标注流水线实现了可扩展的、几何感知的地面真值构建，无需人工标注，为越野导航研究提供了大规模多模态数据集和基准测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable off-road navigation requires accurate estimation of traversable regions and robust perception under diverse terrain and sensing conditions. However, existing datasets lack both scalability and multi-modality, which limits progress in 3D traversability prediction. In this work, we introduce STONE, a large-scale multi-modal dataset for off-road navigation. STONE provides (1) trajectory-guided 3D traversability maps generated by a fully automated, annotation-free pipeline, and (2) comprehensive surround-view sensing with synchronized 128-channel LiDAR, six RGB cameras, and three 4D imaging radars. The dataset covers a wide range of environments and conditions, including day and night, grasslands, farmlands, construction sites, and lakes. Our auto-labeling pipeline reconstructs dense terrain surfaces from LiDAR scans, extracts geometric attributes such as slope, elevation, and roughness, and assigns traversability labels beyond the robot's trajectory using a Mahalanobis-distance-based criterion. This design enables scalable, geometry-aware ground-truth construction without manual annotation. Finally, we establish a benchmark for voxel-level 3D traversability prediction and provide strong baselines under both single-modal and multi-modal settings. STONE is available at: https://konyul.github.io/STONE-dataset/

</details>


### [74] [Robust Spatiotemporal Motion Planning for Multi-Agent Autonomous Racing via Topological Gap Identification and Accelerated MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09188)
*Mingyi Zhang,Cheng Hu,Yiqin Wang,Haotong Qin,Hongye Su,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出拓扑间隙识别与加速MPC框架，用于高速多智能体自动驾驶赛车，通过预测对手行为、构建动态占用走廊选择超车间隙，使用线性时变MPC确保运动学可行性，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速多智能体自动驾驶赛车需要鲁棒的时空规划和精确控制，但现有方法要么过度简化交互，要么放弃严格的运动学约束，无法在严格计算限制下实现高性能。

Method: 提出拓扑间隙识别与加速MPC框架：1) 使用SGPs预测对手行为；2) 构建动态占用走廊以鲁棒选择最优超车间隙；3) 采用线性时变MPC确保严格运动学可行性；4) 使用定制化伪瞬态连续求解器实现高频执行。

Result: 在F1TENTH平台上实验表明：1) 在顺序场景中减少总机动时间51.6%；2) 在密集瓶颈场景中保持超车成功率超过81%；3) 降低平均计算延迟20.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合拓扑间隙识别和加速MPC，在保持严格运动学约束的同时实现了高性能的高速多智能体自动驾驶赛车，显著超越了现有基准方法。

Abstract: High-speed multi-agent autonomous racing demands robust spatiotemporal planning and precise control under strict computational limits. Current methods often oversimplify interactions or abandon strict kinematic constraints. We resolve this by proposing a Topological Gap Identification and Accelerated MPC framework. By predicting opponent behaviors via SGPs, our method constructs dynamic occupancy corridors to robustly select optimal overtaking gaps. We ensure strict kinematic feasibility using a Linear Time-Varying MPC powered by a customized Pseudo-Transient Continuation (PTC) solver for high-frequency execution. Experimental results on the F1TENTH platform show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines: it reduces total maneuver time by 51.6% in sequential scenarios, consistently maintains an overtaking success rate exceeding 81% in dense bottlenecks, and lowers average computational latency by 20.3%, pushing the boundaries of safe and high-speed autonomous racing.

</details>


### [75] [WESPR: Wind-adaptive Energy-Efficient Safe Perception & Planning for Robust Flight with Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09194)
*Khuzema Habib,Pranav Deshakulkarni Manjunath,Kasra Torshizi,Troi Williams,Pratap Tokekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WESPR是一个快速预测环境几何对局部风况影响的框架，通过轻量级管道集成几何感知和局部天气数据，实现主动路径规划和控制适应，在10秒内完成风场估计、路径计算和控制调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部风况显著影响无人机性能，现有自适应控制器虽能缓解湍流影响，但无法感知产生湍流的周围几何环境，无法实现主动规避。现有建模风与环境相互作用的方法通常依赖计算昂贵的流体动力学模拟，限制了实时适应新环境和条件的能力。

Method: WESPR框架通过轻量级管道集成几何感知和局部天气数据，快速预测环境几何对局部风况的影响，包括风场估计、成本效益路径计算和控制策略调整，整个流程在10秒内完成。

Result: 在Crazyflie无人机导航湍流障碍课程上的验证表明，相比风无关的自适应控制器，WESPR实现了最大轨迹偏差减少12.5-58.7%，稳定性提高24.6%。

Conclusion: WESPR框架通过快速预测环境几何对风况的影响，实现了主动路径规划和控制适应，显著提高了无人机在复杂风环境中的导航性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Local wind conditions strongly influence drone performance: headwinds increase flight time, crosswinds and wind shear hinder agility in cluttered spaces, while tailwinds reduce travel time. Although adaptive controllers can mitigate turbulence, they remain unaware of the surrounding geometry that generates it, preventing proactive avoidance. Existing methods that model how wind interacts with the environment typically rely on computationally expensive fluid dynamics simulations, limiting real-time adaptation to new environments and conditions. To bridge this gap, we present WESPR, a fast framework that predicts how environmental geometry affects local wind conditions, enabling proactive path planning and control adaptation. Our lightweight pipeline integrates geometric perception and local weather data to estimate wind fields, compute cost-efficient paths, and adjust control strategies-all within 10 seconds. We validate WESPR on a Crazyflie drone navigating turbulent obstacle courses. Our results show a 12.5-58.7% reduction in maximum trajectory deviation and a 24.6% improvement in stability compared to a wind-agnostic adaptive controller.

</details>


### [76] [TRIP-Bag: A Portable Teleoperation System for Plug-and-Play Robotic Arms and Leaders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09226)
*Noboru Myers,Sankalp Yamsani,Obin Kwon,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRIP-Bag是一个便携式遥操作系统，用于在多样化环境中收集高质量机器人操作数据，解决了现有数据收集方法中的体现差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的机器人策略面临大规模多样化操作数据收集的挑战。现有的野外数据收集方法通常依赖基于视觉的手持夹爪或手套姿态估计，这引入了收集平台与目标机器人之间的体现差距。遥操作系统虽然消除了体现差距，但通常难以在实验室环境外部署。

Method: 提出TRIP-Bag（便携包中的遥操作、记录与智能），这是一个完全包含在商业手提箱内的便携式木偶式遥操作系统。系统设置时间少于5分钟，采用直接关节到关节的遥操作方式，能够在任何环境中实现快速可靠的数据收集。

Result: 通过非专家用户的实验验证了TRIP-Bag的可用性，表明系统直观易用。通过训练基准操作策略确认了收集数据的质量，证明了其作为机器人学习实用资源的价值。

Conclusion: TRIP-Bag为在多样化环境中收集高保真操作数据提供了一个实用的解决方案，解决了现有数据收集方法的局限性，为机器人学习提供了高质量的数据资源。

Abstract: Large scale, diverse demonstration data for manipulation tasks remains a major challenge in learning-based robot policies. Existing in-the-wild data collection approaches often rely on vision-based pose estimation of hand-held grippers or gloves, which introduces an embodiment gap between the collection platform and the target robot. Teleoperation systems eliminate the embodiment gap, but are typically impractical to deploy outside the laboratory environment. We propose TRIP-Bag (Teleoperation, Recording, Intelligence in a Portable Bag), a portable, puppeteer-style teleoperation system fully contained within a commercial suitcase, as a practical solution for collecting high-fidelity manipulation data across varied settings. With a setup time of under five minutes and direct joint-to-joint teleoperation, TRIP-Bag enables rapid and reliable data collection in any environment. We validated TRIP-Bag's usability through experiments with non-expert users, showing that the system is intuitive and easy to operate. Furthermore, we confirmed the quality of the collected data by training benchmark manipulation policies, demonstrating its value as a practical resource for robot learning.

</details>


### [77] [MO-Playground: Massively Parallelized Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09237)
*Neil Janwani,Ellen Novoseller,Vernon J. Lawhern,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MORLAX算法和MO-Playground环境库，通过GPU并行化实现多目标强化学习25-270倍加速，应用于BRUCE人形机器人六目标优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MORL算法无法有效利用大规模并行计算（如GPU），导致计算时间过长，限制了在多目标机器人复杂问题中的应用

Method: 开发MORLAX（GPU原生快速MORL算法）和MO-Playground（GPU加速多目标环境库），支持大规模并行环境模拟

Result: 在几分钟内近似帕累托集，相比传统CPU方法实现25-270倍加速，同时获得更优的帕累托前沿超体积

Conclusion: 通过GPU并行化显著提升MORL计算效率，成功应用于BRUCE人形机器人的六目标运动策略学习，展示了方法的通用性

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is a powerful tool to learn Pareto-optimal policy families across conflicting objectives. However, unlike traditional RL algorithms, existing MORL algorithms do not effectively leverage large-scale parallelization to concurrently simulate thousands of environments, resulting in vastly increased computation time. Ultimately, this has limited MORL's application towards complex multi-objective robotics problems. To address these challenges, we present 1) MORLAX, a new GPU-native, fast MORL algorithm, and 2) MO-Playground, a pip-installable playground of GPU-accelerated multi-objective environments. Together, MORLAX and MO-Playground approximate Pareto sets within minutes, offering 25-270x speed-ups compared to legacy CPU-based approaches whilst achieving superior Pareto front hypervolumes. We demonstrate the versatility of our approach by implementing a custom BRUCE humanoid robot environment using MO-Playground and learning Pareto-optimal locomotion policies across 6 realistic objectives for BRUCE, such as smoothness, efficiency and arm swinging.

</details>


### [78] [See, Plan, Rewind: Progress-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09292)
*Tingjun Dai,Mingfei Han,Tingwen Du,Zhiheng Liu,Zhihui Li,Salman Khan,Jun Yu,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPR是一个基于视觉-语言-动作的进度感知框架，通过"观察-规划-回滚"循环将语言指令动态分解为空间子目标，实现鲁棒的机器人操作和错误恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作任务中缺乏对任务进度的显式测量和监控能力，这限制了模型对当前任务状态的感知、可验证中间状态的预测以及失败时的检测和恢复能力。需要一种能够动态将语言指令分解为空间子目标并监控进度的框架。

Method: SPR框架采用"观察-规划-回滚"的核心循环：1) 观察当前状态和即将到来的里程碑；2) 规划到下一个2D路点的轨迹；3) 通过监控进度与预期序列的对比，在失败时回滚到可恢复状态。这是一个无需额外训练数据或辅助模型的闭环方法。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，SPR比MolmoAct基线高出5%。在具有未见指令和初始状态的挑战性LIBERO-Plus基准测试中，SPR实现了最先进的鲁棒性，性能下降最小，超越了OpenVLA-OFT和UniVLA，展示了卓越的分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPR框架通过显式的进度监控和动态子目标分解，实现了鲁棒的机器人操作和错误恢复。其闭环的"观察-规划-回滚"方法在多个基准测试中表现出优异的泛化能力和鲁棒性，特别是在分布外场景下。

Abstract: Measurement of task progress through explicit, actionable milestones is critical for robust robotic manipulation. This progress awareness enables a model to ground its current task status, anticipate verifiable intermediate states, and detect and recover from failures when progress stalls. To embody this capability, we introduce See, Plan, Rewind (SPR), a progress-aware vision-language-action framework that dynamically grounds language instructions into a sequence of spatial subgoals. SPR operates through a continuous core cycle, Seeing the current state and upcoming milestone, Planning a trajectory towards the next 2D waypoint, and Rewinding to a recoverable state upon failure by monitoring progress against the expected sequence. This closed-loop approach enables robust error correction without requiring additional training data or auxiliary models. Extensive experiments demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, generalization and robustness: SPR outperforms the MolmoAct baseline by 5\% on the LIBERO benchmark. On the challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark with unseen instructions and initial states, SPR achieves state-of-the-art robustness with the smallest performance drop, surpassing OpenVLA-OFT and UniVLA, demonstrating superior out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [79] [NLiPsCalib: An Efficient Calibration Framework for High-Fidelity 3D Reconstruction of Curved Visuotactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09319)
*Xuhao Qin,Feiyu Zhao,Yatao Leng,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NLiPsCalib：一种用于曲面视觉触觉传感器的物理一致高效校准框架，通过集成可控近场光源和近光光度立体视觉技术，简化校准过程，仅需与日常物体进行少量简单接触即可完成校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物仿生曲面视觉触觉传感器虽能增强传感器运动能力，但其感知质量常因非均匀照明而降低，导致重建精度下降且通常需要校准。现有校准方法依赖定制压头和专用设备收集大规模光度数据，过程昂贵且劳动密集。

Method: 提出NLiPsCalib框架，集成可控近场光源并利用近光光度立体视觉技术估计接触几何形状。开发了NLiPsTac传感器进行验证，将校准简化为与日常物体的少量简单接触。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够通过简单校准程序在不同曲面形态上实现高保真3D重建，显著降低了开发定制化视觉触觉传感器的门槛。

Conclusion: NLiPsCalib框架通过物理一致且高效的校准方法，使视觉触觉传感技术更易于被广泛社区采用，促进了多样化几何形状定制传感器的开发。

Abstract: Recent advances in visuotactile sensors increasingly employ biomimetic curved surfaces to enhance sensorimotor capabilities. Although such curved visuotactile sensors enable more conformal object contact, their perceptual quality is often degraded by non-uniform illumination, which reduces reconstruction accuracy and typically necessitates calibration. Existing calibration methods commonly rely on customized indenters and specialized devices to collect large-scale photometric data, but these processes are expensive and labor-intensive. To overcome these calibration challenges, we present NLiPsCalib, a physics-consistent and efficient calibration framework for curved visuotactile sensors. NLiPsCalib integrates controllable near-field light sources and leverages Near-Light Photometric Stereo (NLiPs) to estimate contact geometry, simplifying calibration to just a few simple contacts with everyday objects. We further introduce NLiPsTac, a controllable-light-source tactile sensor developed to validate our framework. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach enables high-fidelity 3D reconstruction across diverse curved form factors with a simple calibration procedure. We emphasize that our approach lowers the barrier to developing customized visuotactile sensors of diverse geometries, thereby making visuotactile sensing more accessible to the broader community.

</details>


### [80] [From Flow to One Step: Real-Time Multi-Modal Trajectory Policies via Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation-based Distribution Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09415)
*Ju Dong,Liding Zhang,Lei Zhang,Yu Fu,Kaixin Bai,Zoltan-Csaba Marton,Zhenshan Bing,Zhaopeng Chen,Alois Christian Knoll,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于隐式最大似然估计的单步蒸馏框架，将条件流匹配专家模型转化为快速单步学生模型，实现高频闭环控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成策略在机器人操作中表现出色，但依赖迭代ODE积分导致延迟高，限制了高频闭环控制。现有单步加速方法存在分布坍缩问题，产生平均化轨迹而无法执行连贯操作策略

Method: 使用隐式最大似然估计将条件流匹配专家模型蒸馏为快速单步学生模型；采用双向Chamfer距离作为集合级目标，促进模态覆盖和保真度；统一感知编码器整合多视角RGB、深度、点云和本体感知信息

Result: 实现了高频控制，支持实时滚动时域重规划，在动态扰动下提高了鲁棒性，同时保持了教师模型的多模态动作分布

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了生成策略的延迟问题，在保持多模态分布的同时实现单步推理，为实时机器人操作控制提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation by modeling multi-modal human demonstrations. However, their reliance on iterative Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration introduces substantial latency, limiting high-frequency closed-loop control. Recent single-step acceleration methods alleviate this overhead but often exhibit distributional collapse, producing averaged trajectories that fail to execute coherent manipulation strategies. We propose a framework that distills a Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) expert into a fast single-step student via Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE). A bi-directional Chamfer distance provides a set-level objective that promotes both mode coverage and fidelity, enabling preservation of the teacher multi-modal action distribution in a single forward pass. A unified perception encoder further integrates multi-view RGB, depth, point clouds, and proprioception into a geometry-aware representation. The resulting high-frequency control supports real-time receding-horizon re-planning and improved robustness under dynamic disturbances.

</details>


### [81] [Stein Variational Ergodic Surface Coverage with SE(3) Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09458)
*Jiayun Li,Yufeng Jin,Sangli Teng,Dejian Gong,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于SE(3)流形感知的Stein变分梯度下降方法，用于机器人表面覆盖轨迹生成，解决了现有方法在点云目标和SE(3)约束处理上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遍历轨迹优化方法在覆盖任务中表现良好，但在处理点云目标时面临非凸优化景观和SE(3)约束处理不足的问题，特别是在采样即优化技术中。

Method: 提出预处理的SE(3) Stein变分梯度下降方法：1) 将点云遍历覆盖重新表述为流形感知采样问题；2) 推导SE(3)特定的SVGD粒子更新；3) 开发预处理器加速轨迹优化收敛。

Result: 在3D点云表面覆盖基准测试和机器人表面绘制任务中，该方法相比现有TO和SAO方法获得更优的覆盖质量，计算量可控，并在真实机器人实验中验证有效。

Conclusion: 提出的采样框架能够一致地找到比现有优化方法和SAO基线更优的局部最优解，同时保持SE(3)几何结构，为机器人表面操作任务提供了有效的轨迹生成方案。

Abstract: Surface manipulation tasks require robots to generate trajectories that comprehensively cover complex 3D surfaces while maintaining precise end-effector poses. Existing ergodic trajectory optimization (TO) methods demonstrate success in coverage tasks, while struggling with point-cloud targets due to the nonconvex optimization landscapes and the inadequate handling of SE(3) constraints in sampling-as-optimization (SAO) techniques. In this work, we introduce a preconditioned SE(3) Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD) approach for SAO ergodic trajectory generation. Our proposed approach comprises multiple innovations. First, we reformulate point-cloud ergodic coverage as a manifold-aware sampling problem. Second, we derive SE(3)-specific SVGD particle updates, and, third, we develop a preconditioner to accelerate TO convergence. Our sampling-based framework consistently identifies superior local optima compared to strong optimization-based and SAO baselines while preserving the SE(3) geometric structure. Experiments on a 3D point-cloud surface coverage benchmark and robotic surface drawing tasks demonstrate that our method achieves superior coverage quality with tractable computation in our setting relative to existing TO and SAO approaches, and is validated in real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [82] [SEA-Nav: Efficient Policy Learning for Safe and Agile Quadruped Navigation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09460)
*Shiyi Chen,Mingye Yang,Haiyan Mao,Jiaqi Zhang,Haiyi Liu,Shuheng He,Debing Zhang,Zihao Qiu,Chun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEA-Nav是一个用于四足机器人导航的强化学习框架，通过可微控制屏障函数确保安全，自适应碰撞回放机制加速学习，在密集障碍环境中实现分钟级训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人导航方法存在局限性：在简单障碍分布中缺乏安全性和敏捷性，在复杂环境中运动缓慢，且需要过长的训练时间。需要一种能在密集障碍环境中高效训练的安全、高效、敏捷导航解决方案。

Method: 1. 使用可微控制屏障函数(CBF)作为防护罩，约束导航策略输出安全速度命令；2. 引入自适应碰撞回放机制和危险探索奖励，增加从关键经验中学习的概率；3. 结合运动学动作约束确保安全速度命令，便于物理部署。

Result: 该方法首次在现实世界中实现了具有挑战性的四足机器人导航，且训练时间仅为分钟级别，在密集障碍环境中表现出安全、高效和敏捷的导航性能。

Conclusion: SEA-Nav框架通过结合CBF安全约束、自适应学习机制和运动学约束，成功解决了四足机器人在密集障碍环境中导航的训练效率和安全问题，实现了现实世界中的快速部署。

Abstract: Efficiently training quadruped robot navigation in densely cluttered environments remains a significant challenge. Existing methods are either limited by a lack of safety and agility in simple obstacle distributions or suffer from slow locomotion in complex environments, often requiring excessively long training phases. To this end, we propose SEA-Nav (Safe, Efficient, and Agile Navigation), a reinforcement learning framework for quadruped navigation. Within diverse and dense obstacle environments, a differentiable control barrier function (CBF)-based shield constraints the navigation policy to output safe velocity commands. An adaptive collision replay mechanism and hazardous exploration rewards are introduced to increase the probability of learning from critical experiences, guiding efficient exploration and exploitation. Finally, kinematic action constraints are incorporated to ensure safe velocity commands, facilitating successful physical deployment. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach that achieves highly challenging quadruped navigation in the real world with minute-level training time.

</details>


### [83] [Receptogenesis in a Vascularized Robotic Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09473)
*Kadri-Ann Pankratov,Leonid Zinatullin,Hans Priks,Adele Metsniit,Urmas Johanson,Tarmo Tamm,Alvo Aabloo,Edoardo Sinibaldi,Indrek Must*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用流体血管系统实现机器人原位材料合成与传感器构建，通过光聚合反应从内部流体储备中按需生成传感器，实现机器人的物理适应性进化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器人系统赋予在运行过程中生成新硬件的能力，实现物理适应性控制。不同于依赖部署前后离散组件集成的模块化系统，本研究旨在通过动态材料重构来塑造身体的内在功能，实现物理适应与发展

Method: 采用血管化机器人复合材料，通过协调流体前体运输与外部局部UV照射，驱动原位光聚合反应，从内向外化学重构血管系统。该反应将含有光潜伏引发剂的前体转化为UV敏感聚吡咯固体分散体，建立传感模态

Result: 成功实现了receptogenesis（受体生成）——基于环境线索从内部流体储备按需构建传感器。新合成的传感器通过特征性电阻抗降低得到验证，并用于闭环控制飞蛾仿生机器人的翅膀拍打，实时增强了机器人能力

Conclusion: 建立了基于材料的构成性进化框架，使机器人能够在复杂环境中物理生长支持新兴行为所需的硬件，为自主系统生成专门化特征（如情境机器人中的神经血管系统）提供了途径

Abstract: Equipping robotic systems with the capacity to generate $\textit{ex novo}$ hardware during operation extends control of physical adaptability. Unlike modular systems that rely on discrete component integration pre- or post-deployment, we envision the possibility that physical adaptation and development emerge from dynamic material restructuring to shape the body's intrinsic functions. Drawing inspiration from circulatory systems that redistribute mass and function in biological organisms, we utilize fluidics to restructure the material interface, a capability currently unpaired in robotics. Here, we realize this synthetic growth capability through a vascularized robotic composite designed for programmable material synthesis, demonstrated via receptogenesis - the on-demand construction of sensors from internal fluid reserves based on environmental cues. By coordinating the fluidic transport of precursors with external localized UV irradiation, we drive an $\textit{in situ}$ photopolymerization that chemically reconstructs the vasculature from the inside out. This reaction converts precursors with photolatent initiator into a solid dispersion of UV-sensitive polypyrrole, establishing a sensing modality validated by a characteristic decrease in electrical impedance. The newly synthesized sensor closed a control loop to regulate wing flapping in a moth-inspired robotic demonstrator. This physical update increased the robot's capability in real time. This work establishes a materials-based framework for constitutive evolution, enabling robots to physically grow the hardware needed to support emerging behaviors in a complex environment; for example, suggesting a pathway toward autonomous systems capable of generating specialized features, such as neurovascular systems in situated robotics.

</details>


### [84] [StyleVLA: Driving Style-Aware Vision Language Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09482)
*Yuan Gao,Dengyuan Hua,Mattia Piccinini,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Korbinian Moller,Lin Li,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StyleVLA是一个物理感知的视觉语言动作模型，用于生成多样化且物理可行的驾驶行为，通过混合损失函数和大型指令数据集训练，在自动驾驶任务中超越闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言动作模型主要生成通用的无碰撞轨迹，但缺乏对多样化驾驶风格的适应能力，且将轨迹生成视为简单的token预测，可能产生运动学上不可行的动作。需要解决个性化驾驶和物理可行性问题。

Method: 提出StyleVLA框架，引入混合损失函数结合运动学一致性约束和连续回归头来提升轨迹可行性。基于Qwen3-VL-4B构建，使用包含1.2k场景、76k鸟瞰图和42k第一人称视角样本的大型指令数据集，包含五种驾驶风格的真实轨迹和自然语言指令。

Result: StyleVLA在综合驾驶评分（成功率、物理可行性、风格一致性）上显著优于专有模型和SOTA VLA模型：鸟瞰图0.55 vs Gemini-3-Pro的0.32，第一人称视角0.51 vs 0.35。4B参数模型在领域特定任务上超越闭源模型。

Conclusion: 专门化、物理感知的轻量级模型可以在领域特定任务上超越闭源模型，为自动驾驶中的个性化驾驶行为生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) bridge visual perception and linguistic reasoning. In Autonomous Driving (AD), this synergy has enabled Vision Language Action (VLA) models, which translate high-level multimodal understanding into driving behaviors, typically represented as future trajectories. However, existing VLA models mainly generate generic collision-free trajectories. Beyond collision avoidance, adapting to diverse driving styles (e.g., sporty, comfortable) is essential for personalized driving. Moreover, many methods treat trajectory generation as naive token prediction, which can produce kinematically infeasible actions. To address these limitations, we present StyleVLA, a physics-informed VLA framework for generating diverse and physically plausible driving behaviors. We introduce a hybrid loss that combines a kinematic consistency constraint with a continuous regression head to improve trajectory feasibility. To train StyleVLA, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, we construct a large-scale instruction dataset with over 1.2k scenarios, 76k Bird's Eye View (BEV) samples, and 42k First Person View (FPV) samples, with ground-truth trajectories for five driving styles and natural-language instructions. Experiments show that our 4B-parameter StyleVLA significantly outperforms proprietary models (e.g., Gemini-3-Pro) and state-of-the-art VLA models. Using a composite driving score measuring success rate, physical feasibility, and style adherence, StyleVLA achieves 0.55 on BEV and 0.51 on FPV, versus 0.32 and 0.35 for Gemini-3-Pro. These results show that a specialized, physics-informed, lightweight model can surpass closed-source models on domain-specific tasks.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Short-Horizon: VQ-Memory for Robust Long-Horizon Manipulation in Non-Markovian Simulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09513)
*Wang Honghui,Jing Zhi,Ao Jicong,Song Shiji,Li Xuelong,Huang Gao,Bai Chenjia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RuleSafe是一个基于LLM辅助仿真框架的关节物体操作基准，包含多种解锁机制的安全箱任务，提出VQ-Memory作为紧凑结构化时序表示来提升长时程规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真基准主要关注简单的拾取放置任务，无法捕捉真实世界任务的非马尔可夫特性和关节物体交互的复杂性，且真实机器人数据收集成本高昂。

Method: 1) 构建RuleSafe基准，包含多种解锁机制（钥匙锁、密码锁、逻辑锁）的安全箱，通过LLM生成非马尔可夫、长时程任务规则；2) 提出VQ-Memory，使用向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）将过去本体感知状态编码为离散潜在标记，形成紧凑结构化时序表示。

Result: 在先进的视觉-语言-动作模型和扩散策略上的大量实验表明，VQ-Memory能持续提升长时程规划能力，增强对未见配置的泛化性，实现更高效的操作并降低计算成本。

Conclusion: RuleSafe基准和VQ-Memory表示方法为解决复杂关节物体操作中的非马尔可夫和长时程规划问题提供了有效方案，为机器人仿真和算法评估建立了更真实的测试平台。

Abstract: The high cost of collecting real-robot data has made robotic simulation a scalable platform for both evaluation and data generation. Yet most existing benchmarks concentrate on simple manipulation tasks such as pick-and-place, failing to capture the non-Markovian characteristics of real-world tasks and the complexity of articulated object interactions. To address this limitation, we present RuleSafe, a new articulated manipulation benchmark built upon a scalable LLM-aided simulation framework. RuleSafe features safes with diverse unlocking mechanisms, such as key locks, password locks, and logic locks, which require different multi-stage reasoning and manipulation strategies. These LLM-generated rules produce non-Markovian and long-horizon tasks that require temporal modeling and memory-based reasoning. We further propose VQ-Memory, a compact and structured temporal representation that uses vector-quantized variational autoencoders (VQ-VAEs) to encode past proprioceptive states into discrete latent tokens. This representation filters low-level noise while preserving high-level task-phase context, providing lightweight yet robust temporal cues that are compatible with existing Vision-Language-Action models (VLA). Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art VLA models and diffusion policies show that VQ-Memory consistently improves long-horizon planning, enhances generalization to unseen configurations, and enables more efficient manipulation with reduced computational cost. Project page: vqmemory.github.io

</details>


### [86] [NS-VLA: Towards Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09542)
*Ziyue Zhu,Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Zhiqiu Zhao,Shengjie Li,Yi Wang,Fang Li,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经符号视觉-语言-动作框架，通过在线强化学习解决VLA模型在可重用基元学习、数据依赖和探索能力方面的挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型面临三个主要挑战：1) 难以学习相关且可重用的基元；2) 过度依赖大规模数据和复杂架构；3) 缺乏超越演示的探索能力。需要一种更高效、更具泛化性的框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出NS-VLA框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 符号编码器将视觉和语言特征嵌入并提取结构化基元；2) 符号求解器实现数据高效的动作序列生成；3) 在线强化学习优化生成过程，支持扩展性探索。

Result: 在机器人操作基准测试中，NS-VLA在一次性训练和数据扰动设置下均优于先前方法，同时展现出优越的零样本泛化能力、高数据效率和扩展的探索空间。

Conclusion: NS-VLA框架通过神经符号方法和在线强化学习的结合，有效解决了VLA模型的关键挑战，为机器人操作任务提供了更高效、更泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are formulated to ground instructions in visual context and generate action sequences for robotic manipulation. Despite recent progress, VLA models still face challenges in learning related and reusable primitives, reducing reliance on large-scale data and complex architectures, and enabling exploration beyond demonstrations. To address these challenges, we propose a novel Neuro-Symbolic Vision-Language-Action (NS-VLA) framework via online reinforcement learning (RL). It introduces a symbolic encoder to embedding vision and language features and extract structured primitives, utilizes a symbolic solver for data-efficient action sequencing, and leverages online RL to optimize generation via expansive exploration. Experiments on robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that NS-VLA outperforms previous methods in both one-shot training and data-perturbed settings, while simultaneously exhibiting superior zero-shot generalizability, high data efficiency and expanded exploration space. Our code is available.

</details>


### [87] [Trajectory Optimization for Self-Wrap-Aware Cable-Towed Planar Object Manipulation under Implicit Tension Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09557)
*Yu Li,Amin Fakhari,Hamid Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种考虑自缠绕的缆绳牵引操纵优化框架，通过路由感知、张力隐式轨迹优化(TITO)耦合张力约束和路由条件传输映射，建立了从严格模式到三种可处理松弛的层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆绳/绳索元素在可变形物体操纵中普遍存在，作为力传递介质，其路由和接触决定了力矩的传递方式。在缆绳牵引操纵中，传递是单向和混合的：缆绳仅在绷紧时能拉动，松弛时无力；实践中缆绳可能接触物体边界并自缠绕边缘，这不仅涉及碰撞避免，还改变了力矩传递通道，通过改变有效作用点和力臂，将路由几何与刚体运动和张力耦合。

Method: 将自缠绕牵引建模为路由感知、张力隐式轨迹优化(TITO)问题，耦合：(i)张力隐式的绷紧/松弛约束；(ii)路由条件的有效长度和力矩传输映射。构建了从严格模式条件参考到三种可处理松弛的层次结构：全模式松弛(FMR)、二元模式松弛(BMR)和隐式模式松弛(IMR)。

Result: 在平面牵引任务中，将路由作为显式决策通常产生保守解，保持在切换边界附近；而IMR通过状态演化诱导自缠绕，并在转向需要时利用重定向的扭矩通道。

Conclusion: 隐式模式松弛(IMR)方法能够通过状态演化自然地诱导自缠绕，有效利用重定向的扭矩通道，相比显式路由决策的保守方法，在转向任务中表现更优，展示了路由几何与刚体运动耦合的重要性。

Abstract: Cable/rope elements are pervasive in deformable-object manipulation, often serving as a deformable force-transmission medium whose routing and contact determine how wrenches are delivered. In cable-towed manipulation, transmission is unilateral and hybrid: the tether can pull only when taut and becomes force-free when slack; in practice, the tether may also contact the object boundary and self-wrap around edges, which is not merely collision avoidance but a change of the wrench transmission channel by shifting the effective application point and moment arm, thereby coupling routing geometry with rigid-body motion and tensioning. We formulate self-wrap towing as a routing-aware, tensioning-implicit trajectory optimization (TITO) problem that couples (i) a tensioning-implicit taut/slack constraint and (ii) routing-conditioned transmission maps for effective length and wrench, and we build a relaxation hierarchy from a strict mode-conditioned reference to three tractable relaxations: Full-Mode Relaxation (FMR), Binary-Mode Relaxation (BMR), and Implicit-Mode Relaxation (IMR). Across planar towing tasks, we find that making routing an explicit decision often yields conservative solutions that stay near switching boundaries, whereas IMR induces self-wrap through state evolution and exploits the redirected torque channel whenever turning requires it.

</details>


### [88] [SCDP: Learning Humanoid Locomotion from Partial Observations via Mixed-Observation Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09574)
*Milo Carroll,Tianhu Peng,Lingfan Bao,Chengxu Zhou,Zhibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCDP是一种仅使用机载传感器实现人形机器人运动控制的方法，无需显式状态估计，通过传感器条件扩散策略和混合观测训练实现部署


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖特权全身状态，需要复杂且不可靠的状态估计，难以将离线数据集中的运动控制知识蒸馏为可部署策略

Method: 传感器条件扩散策略（SCDP）：1）混合观测训练：扩散模型以传感器历史为条件，监督预测特权未来状态-动作轨迹；2）限制去噪；3）上下文分布对齐；4）上下文感知注意力掩码

Result: 仿真中速度控制成功率99-100%，AMASS测试集跟踪成功率93%，性能与特权基线相当；在真实G1人形机器人上以50Hz部署，无需外部传感或状态估计

Conclusion: SCDP成功实现了仅使用机载传感器的人形机器人运动控制，消除了对显式状态估计的依赖，为实际部署提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Distilling humanoid locomotion control from offline datasets into deployable policies remains a challenge, as existing methods rely on privileged full-body states that require complex and often unreliable state estimation. We present Sensor-Conditioned Diffusion Policies (SCDP) that enables humanoid locomotion using only onboard sensors, eliminating the need for explicit state estimation. SCDP decouples sensing from supervision through mixed-observation training: diffusion model conditions on sensor histories while being supervised to predict privileged future state-action trajectories, enforcing the model to infer the motion dynamics under partial observability. We further develop restricted denoising, context distribution alignment, and context-aware attention masking to encourage implicit state estimation within the model and to prevent train-deploy mismatch. We validate SCDP on velocity-commanded locomotion and motion reference tracking tasks. In simulation, SCDP achieves near-perfect success on velocity control (99-100%) and 93% tracking success in AMASS test set, performing comparable to privileged baselines while using only onboard sensors. Finally, we deploy the trained policy on a real G1 humanoid at 50 Hz, demonstrating robust real robot locomotion without external sensing or state estimation.

</details>


### [89] [Towards Terrain-Aware Safe Locomotion for Quadrupedal Robots Using Proprioceptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09585)
*Peiyu Yang,Jiatao Ding,Wei Pan,Claudio Semini,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体感知的四足机器人安全运动框架，包含2.5维地形估计和基于控制障碍函数的安全控制，无需外部传感器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人在不平坦地形上的安全运动问题，特别是对于仅配备本体感知传感器（IMU、关节编码器、接触力传感器）的低成本机器人，需要可靠的估计和基于估计信息的安全关键控制

Method: 1) 提出估计框架：生成2.5维地形图并提取支撑平面参数，集成到接触和状态估计中；2) 将估计框架集成到安全关键控制管道中：通过制定控制障碍函数提供严格的安全保证

Result: 1) 地形估计方法提供平滑的地形表示；2) 地形、状态和接触的耦合估计框架相比解耦框架：基座位置估计的平均绝对误差减少64.8%，估计方差减少47.2%，接触估计鲁棒性提高；3) 地形感知的CBFs仅依赖本体感知，通过整合历史地形信息和当前测量确保全局安全（避开危险区域）和局部安全（防止身体-地形碰撞）

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种完整的基于本体感知的四足机器人安全运动框架，通过耦合地形估计与安全控制，在仅使用低成本传感器的情况下实现了可靠的地形感知和严格的安全保证，为四足机器人在复杂地形中的实际应用提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Achieving safe quadrupedal locomotion in real-world environments has attracted much attention in recent years. When walking over uneven terrain, achieving reliable estimation and realising safety-critical control based on the obtained information is still an open question. To address this challenge, especially for low-cost robots equipped solely with proprioceptive sensors (e.g., IMUs, joint encoders, and contact force sensors), this work first presents an estimation framework that generates a 2.5-D terrain map and extracts support plane parameters, which are then integrated into contact and state estimation. Then, we integrate this estimation framework into a safety-critical control pipeline by formulating control barrier functions that provide rigorous safety guarantees. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed terrain estimation method provides smooth terrain representations. Moreover, the coupled estimation framework of terrain, state, and contact reduces the mean absolute error of base position estimation by 64.8%, decreases the estimation variance by 47.2%, and improves the robustness of contact estimation compared to a decoupled framework. The terrain-informed CBFs integrate historical terrain information and current proprioceptive measurements to ensure global safety by keeping the robot out of hazardous areas and local safety by preventing body-terrain collision, relying solely on proprioceptive sensing.

</details>


### [90] [A Generalized Voronoi Graph based Coverage Control Approach for Non-Convex Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09596)
*Zuyi Guo,Ronghao Zheng,Meiqin Liu,Senlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于广义Voronoi图的多机器人覆盖控制方法，包含负载均衡算法和协作覆盖两个阶段，用于非凸多障碍物区域的高效覆盖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人系统在非凸区域（含多个障碍物）中实现高效覆盖的挑战。传统方法在复杂非凸环境中存在覆盖效率低、负载不均衡等问题。

Method: 基于广义Voronoi图(GVG)的两阶段方法：1)负载均衡算法阶段：使用GVG将非凸区域划分为多个子区域，开发加权负载均衡算法考虑子区域质量差异，通过迭代优化机器人分配比例实现负载平衡；2)协作覆盖阶段：设计新控制器控制每个机器人有效覆盖区域。

Result: 证明了方法的收敛性，并通过仿真评估了性能。结果表明该方法能有效实现非凸多障碍物区域的覆盖，提高了覆盖效率和负载均衡性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于GVG的两阶段覆盖控制方法能有效解决多机器人在非凸多障碍物区域中的覆盖问题，通过负载均衡和协作控制提高了覆盖效率。

Abstract: To address the challenge of efficient coverage by multi-robot systems in non-convex regions with multiple obstacles, this paper proposes a coverage control method based on the Generalized Voronoi Graph (GVG), which has two phases: Load-Balancing Algorithm phase and Collaborative Coverage phase. In Load-Balancing Algorithm phase, the non-convex region is partitioned into multiple sub-regions based on GVG. Besides, a weighted load-balancing algorithm is developed, which considers the quality differences among sub-regions. By iteratively optimizing the robot allocation ratio, the number of robots in each sub-region is matched with the sub-region quality to achieve load balance. In Collaborative Coverage phase, each robot is controlled by a new controller to effectively coverage the region. The convergence of the method is proved and its performance is evaluated through simulations.

</details>


### [91] [DRIFT: Dual-Representation Inter-Fusion Transformer for Automated Driving Perception with 4D Radar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09695)
*Siqi Pei,Andras Palffy,Dariu M. Gavrila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIFT：一种用于4D雷达点云的双路径架构，通过点路径和柱体路径分别捕获局部和全局特征，并通过特征共享层融合，在目标检测和自由道路估计任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达相比激光雷达点云密度显著更低，需要充分利用局部和全局上下文场景信息来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出双路径架构：点路径聚合细粒度局部特征，柱体路径编码粗粒度全局特征；通过多阶段特征共享层交织两个并行路径，充分利用两种表示。

Result: 在View-of-Delft数据集和内部专有数据集上评估，在目标检测和/或自由道路估计任务上优于基线。例如，在VoD数据集上达到52.6% mAP（CenterPoint为45.4%）。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过有效融合局部和全局上下文，提升了4D雷达点云处理性能，为低成本、鲁棒的自动驾驶感知系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: 4D radars, which provide 3D point cloud data along with Doppler velocity, are attractive components of modern automated driving systems due to their low cost and robustness under adverse weather conditions. However, they provide a significantly lower point cloud density than LiDAR sensors. This makes it important to exploit not only local but also global contextual scene information. This paper proposes DRIFT, a model that effectively captures and fuses both local and global contexts through a dual-path architecture. The model incorporates a point path to aggregate fine-grained local features and a pillar path to encode coarse-grained global features. These two parallel paths are intertwined via novel feature-sharing layers at multiple stages, enabling full utilization of both representations. DRIFT is evaluated on the widely used View-of-Delft (VoD) dataset and a proprietary internal dataset. It outperforms the baselines on the tasks of object detection and/or free road estimation. For example, DRIFT achieves a mean average precision (mAP) of 52.6\% (compared to, say, 45.4\% of CenterPoint) on the VoD dataset.

</details>


### [92] [Robotic Scene Cloning:Advancing Zero-Shot Robotic Scene Adaptation in Manipulation via Visual Prompt Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09712)
*Binyuan Huang,Yuqing Wen,Yucheng Zhao,Yaosi Hu,Tiancai Wang,Chang Wen Chen,Haoqiang Fan,Zhenzhong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RSC是一种通过编辑现有机器人操作轨迹实现场景特定适应的新方法，利用视觉提示机制和条件注入模块提升零样本能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人模型在现实用户场景中部署困难，零样本能力有限，通常需要大量现场数据收集，需要解决场景特定适应问题

Method: 提出Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC)方法，通过视觉提示机制和精心调优的条件注入模块编辑现有机器人操作轨迹，实现准确且场景一致的样本生成，不仅能转移纹理还能进行适度的形状适应

Result: RSC在各种物体类型上表现出可靠的任务性能，在多种模拟和真实环境中的实验表明，RSC显著提升了目标环境中的策略泛化能力

Conclusion: RSC通过编辑现有轨迹实现场景特定适应，有效解决了预训练模型在现实场景部署中的零样本能力限制问题，为机器人模型的实际应用提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Modern robots can perform a wide range of simple tasks and adapt to diverse scenarios in the well-trained environment. However, deploying pre-trained robot models in real-world user scenarios remains challenging due to their limited zero-shot capabilities, often necessitating extensive on-site data collection. To address this issue, we propose Robotic Scene Cloning (RSC), a novel method designed for scene-specific adaptation by editing existing robot operation trajectories. RSC achieves accurate and scene-consistent sample generation by leveraging a visual prompting mechanism and a carefully tuned condition injection module. Not only transferring textures but also performing moderate shape adaptations in response to the visual prompts, RSC demonstrates reliable task performance across a variety of object types. Experiments across various simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that RSC significantly enhances policy generalization in target environments.

</details>


### [93] [Let's Reward Step-by-Step: Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment for Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09740)
*Haoyuan Li,Rui Liu,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SACA框架通过感知基础步感知审计器提取密集监督，解决VLN-CE中复合错误和稀疏奖励问题，实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLN-CE训练范式在泛化能力、错误恢复和训练稳定性之间存在平衡困难：SFT方法存在复合错误问题，难以从分布外状态恢复；RFT方法受限于稀疏结果奖励，二元反馈无法为单个步骤分配信用，导致失败主导批次中的梯度信号崩溃。

Method: 提出Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA)框架：1) Perception-Grounded Step-Aware审计器逐步评估进展，将失败轨迹解耦为有效前缀和精确分歧点；2) Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction机制动态路由批次到专门的重新采样和优化策略，从非完美轨迹中提取密集监督。

Result: 在VLN-CE基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SACA实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SACA框架通过提取密集监督有效解决了VLN-CE中的复合错误和稀疏奖励问题，为视觉语言导航在连续环境中的训练提供了更稳定和有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to learn complex reasoning from long-horizon human interactions. While Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have driven recent progress, current training paradigms struggle to balance generalization capability, error recovery and training stability. Specifically, (i) policies derived from SFT suffer from compounding errors, struggling to recover from out-of-distribution states, and (ii) Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) methods e.g. GRPO are bottlenecked by sparse outcome rewards. Their binary feedback fails to assign credit to individual steps, leading to gradient signal collapse in failure dominant batches. To address these challenges, we introduce Step-Aware Contrastive Alignment (SACA), a framework designed to extract dense supervision from imperfect trajectories. At its core, the Perception-Grounded Step-Aware auditor evaluates progress step-by-step, disentangling failed trajectories into valid prefixes and exact divergence points. Leveraging these signals, Scenario-Conditioned Group Construction mechanism dynamically routes batches to specialized resampling and optimization strategies. Extensive experiments on VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate that SACA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [94] [Caterpillar-Inspired Spring-Based Compressive Continuum Robot for Bristle-based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09745)
*Zhixian Hu,Yu She,Juan Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于弹簧的腱驱动连续体机器人，集成于商用机械臂，用于受限空间检测，结合弯曲与轴向长度变化，集成人工刚毛接触传感器实现表面感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性机器人在管道、导管等受限空间探索中面临空间有限、几何不规则和访问受限的挑战，需要更灵活、紧凑的解决方案

Method: 设计紧凑的弹簧基腱驱动连续体机器人，集成于商用机械臂；采用机械柔顺的连续体结构结合腱驱动模块，实现弯曲与轴向长度变化的耦合；使用恒定曲率运动学模型进行位置控制；集成人工刚毛接触传感器实现表面感知

Result: 在提出的模型和控制管道下，平均位置误差为4.32毫米；成功演示了通过接触交互实现表面感知和受限空间探索

Conclusion: 该紧凑柔顺设计为商用机器人提供了经济有效的升级方案，有望在挑战性环境中实现有效探索，将系统从单纯运动扩展到检测功能

Abstract: Exploration of confined spaces, such as pipelines and ducts, remains challenging for conventional rigid robots due to limited space, irregular geometry, and restricted access. Inspired by caterpillar locomotion and sensing, this paper presents a compact spring-based tendon-driven continuum robot that integrates with commercial robotic arms for confined-space inspection. The system combines a mechanically compliant continuum body with a tendon actuation module, enabling coupled bending and axial length change, and uses a constant-curvature kinematic model for positional control. Experiments show a mean position error of 4.32 mm under the proposed model and control pipeline. To extend the system from motion to inspection, we integrate an artificial bristle contact sensor and demonstrate surface perception and confined-space exploration through contact interactions. This compact and compliant design offers a cost-effective upgrade for commercial robots and promises effective exploration in challenging environments.

</details>


### [95] [TIMID: Time-Dependent Mistake Detection in Videos of Robot Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09782)
*Nerea Gallego,Fernando Salanova,Claudio Mannarano,Cristian Mahulea,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TIMID：一种基于视频异常检测的架构，用于检测机器人执行高级任务时的时间相关错误，通过弱监督训练和模拟数据集解决数据稀缺问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测（VAD）框架主要关注单一、低级的运动学或动作失败，难以识别更复杂的时空任务违规，因为这些违规不一定表现为低级执行错误。机器人系统执行越来越复杂的任务序列时，失败方式也越来越多。

Method: 提出TIMID架构，接收视频、任务提示和潜在错误提示作为输入，输出帧级预测判断错误是否存在。采用VAD公式实现弱监督训练（每个视频仅需单个标签）。为解决错误执行数据稀缺问题，引入多机器人模拟数据集，包含受控时间错误和真实执行数据，用于零样本模拟到真实评估。

Result: 实验表明，现成的视觉语言模型（VLMs）缺乏此任务所需的显式时间推理能力，而TIMID框架成功检测了不同类型的时间错误。

Conclusion: TIMID能够有效检测机器人执行高级任务时的时间相关错误，通过弱监督训练和模拟数据集解决了现有方法的局限性，为复杂任务违规检测提供了新方案。

Abstract: As robotic systems execute increasingly difficult task sequences, so does the number of ways in which they can fail. Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) frameworks typically focus on singular, low-level kinematic or action failures, struggling to identify more complex temporal or spatial task violations, because they do not necessarily manifest as low-level execution errors. To address this problem, the main contribution of this paper is a new VAD-inspired architecture, TIMID, which is able to detect robot time-dependent mistakes when executing high-level tasks. Our architecture receives as inputs a video and prompts of the task and the potential mistake, and returns a frame-level prediction in the video of whether the mistake is present or not. By adopting a VAD formulation, the model can be trained with weak supervision, requiring only a single label per video. Additionally, to alleviate the problem of data scarcity of incorrect executions, we introduce a multi-robot simulation dataset with controlled temporal errors and real executions for zero-shot sim-to-real evaluation. Our experiments demonstrate that out-of-the-box VLMs lack the explicit temporal reasoning required for this task, whereas our framework successfully detects different types of temporal errors. Project: https://ropertunizar.github.io/TIMID/

</details>


### [96] [Lightweight 3D LiDAR-Based UAV Tracking: An Adaptive Extended Kalman Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09783)
*Nivand Khosravi,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波的轻量级LiDAR无人机跟踪系统，解决了稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据的挑战，适用于小型无人机在GPS拒止环境下的相对定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的相对定位对于无人机集群协同飞行和避碰至关重要。虽然视觉跟踪已被广泛研究，但基于3D LiDAR的方法在变化光照条件下更鲁棒，却未被充分利用。现有系统通常依赖笨重、高功耗的传感器，不适用于具有严格载荷和能量限制的小型无人机。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级LiDAR无人机跟踪系统，采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波框架。该方法动态调整噪声协方差矩阵（使用创新和残差统计），以增强实际条件下的跟踪精度。系统还包含恢复机制，确保在因散射LiDAR返回或遮挡导致的临时检测失败期间保持跟踪连续性。

Result: 使用安装在DJI F550无人机上的Livox Mid-360 LiDAR进行真实飞行场景实验验证。所提方法在稀疏LiDAR返回和间歇性检测条件下表现出鲁棒的无人机跟踪性能，在激进机动期间始终优于标准卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在GPS拒止环境中实现可靠的相对定位，无需多传感器阵列或外部基础设施。自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波方法有效解决了非重复扫描3D LiDAR产生的稀疏、噪声和非均匀点云数据挑战，同时满足小型无人机的严格载荷限制。

Abstract: Accurate relative positioning is crucial for swarm aerial robotics, enabling coordinated flight and collision avoidance. Although vision-based tracking has been extensively studied, 3D LiDAR-based methods remain underutilized despite their robustness under varying lighting conditions. Existing systems often rely on bulky, power-intensive sensors, making them impractical for small UAVs with strict payload and energy constraints. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-based UAV tracking system incorporating an Adaptive Extended Kalman Filter (AEKF) framework. Our approach effectively addresses the challenges posed by sparse, noisy, and nonuniform point cloud data generated by non-repetitive scanning 3D LiDARs, ensuring reliable tracking while remaining suitable for small drones with strict payload constraints. Unlike conventional filtering techniques, the proposed method dynamically adjusts the noise covariance matrices using innovation and residual statistics, thereby enhancing tracking accuracy under real-world conditions. Additionally, a recovery mechanism ensures continuity of tracking during temporary detection failures caused by scattered LiDAR returns or occlusions. Experimental validation was performed using a Livox Mid-360 LiDAR mounted on a DJI F550 UAV in real-world flight scenarios. The proposed method demonstrated robust UAV tracking performance under sparse LiDAR returns and intermittent detections, consistently outperforming both standard Kalman filtering and particle filtering approaches during aggressive maneuvers. These results confirm that the framework enables reliable relative positioning in GPS-denied environments without the need for multi-sensor arrays or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [97] [Emerging Extrinsic Dexterity in Cluttered Scenes via Dynamics-aware Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09882)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yifan Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Mi Yan,Yuntian Deng,Xuesong Shi,Xiaoguang Zhao,Yizhou Wang,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPL框架，通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导物体动力学表示，用于条件强化学习，实现杂乱环境中非抓取操作的灵巧性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对杂乱环境中多物体交互复杂动力学的显式建模，限制了非抓取操作在实际环境中的应用

Method: DAPL框架：通过显式世界建模学习接触诱导物体动力学表示，将该表示作为条件输入强化学习，无需手工接触启发式或复杂奖励塑形

Result: 在模拟杂乱场景中成功率比抓取操作、人工遥操作和先前基于表示的方法高出25%以上；真实世界10个杂乱场景中成功率约50%，杂货部署展示了稳健的仿真到现实迁移

Conclusion: DAPL框架通过显式建模接触动力学，实现了杂乱环境中非抓取操作的灵巧性，展示了实际应用潜力

Abstract: Extrinsic dexterity leverages environmental contact to overcome the limitations of prehensile manipulation. However, achieving such dexterity in cluttered scenes remains challenging and underexplored, as it requires selectively exploiting contact among multiple interacting objects with inherently coupled dynamics. Existing approaches lack explicit modeling of such complex dynamics and therefore fall short in non-prehensile manipulation in cluttered environments, which in turn limits their practical applicability in real-world environments.
