### [124] [The Black Death Anomaly: A Non-Abelian Field Theory of Epidemiological Safe Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08874)
*Jose de Jesus Bernal-Alvarado,David Delepine*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文将黑死病流行病学嵌入非阿贝尔规范理论框架，通过SU(N)环境规范场耦合地理位移与表型突变，解释了14世纪黑死病快速遗传辐射和地理安全区的历史异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反应-扩散模型无法解释14世纪黑死病中鼠疫耶尔森菌的快速遗传辐射以及中欧等广阔地理安全区的异常存在。这些历史异常需要新的理论框架来统一解释病原体动态与进化过程。

Method: 采用Doi-Peliti形式将多菌株流行病的随机主方程映射为协变经典场论。引入SU(N)环境规范场A_μ，将地理位移与表型突变主动耦合，将进化漂移视为空间输运现象。通过线性稳定性分析研究协变平流驱动的微分流（图灵-霍普夫）不稳定性，并在大N（'t Hooft）连续极限下分析拓扑结构。

Result: 协变平流驱动微分流不稳定性，自发打破空间对称性，产生突变行波。在大N连续极限下，证明历史安全区不是统计异常或完美隔离的结果，而是数学上必要的拓扑空洞。突变波前的相消干涉解析地解析为零阶贝塞尔函数J_0，精确映射到波兰和波希米亚的历史生存模式。

Conclusion: 通过将宏观病原体动力学嵌入非阿贝尔规范理论，成功解释了黑死病的历史异常。该方法为流行病学与进化生物学的交叉研究提供了新的数学框架，将地理扩散与遗传进化统一为单一协变过程。

Abstract: Classical reaction-diffusion models of the 14th-century Black Death fail to explain the rapid genetic radiation of \textit{Yersinia pestis} and the anomalous emergence of vast, untouched geographic safe zones, such as Central Europe. In this work, we resolve these historical anomalies by embedding macroscopic pathogen dynamics within a non-Abelian gauge theory. Utilizing the Doi-Peliti formalism, we map the stochastic master equation of a multi-strain epidemic into a covariant classical field theory. We introduce an $SU(N)$ environmental gauge field, $\mathbf{A}_μ$, which actively couples geographic displacement to phenotypic mutation, treating evolutionary drift as a spatial transport phenomenon. We demonstrate via linear stability analysis that this covariant advection drives a Differential Flow (Turing-Hopf) instability, spontaneously breaking spatial symmetry to generate traveling waves of mutation. Furthermore, by extending the pathogen multiplet to the large-$N$ ('t Hooft) continuum limit, we prove that historical safe zones are not statistical outliers nor the result of perfect quarantine, but are mathematically necessary topological voids. In this continuous limit, the destructive interference of the mutating wavefronts analytically resolves into a stable, isotropic macroscopic node governed by a zeroth-order Bessel function ($J_0$), precisely mapping onto the historical survival of Poland and Bohemia.

</details>


### [125] [Misspecification of the generation time distribution and its impact on Rt estimates in structured populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09451)
*Ioana Bouros,Robin Thompson,David Gavaghan,Ben Lamber*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 研究比较了基于更新方程的两种Rt推断框架：单群体模型和结构化群体模型，分析了群体间世代间隔分布差异对Rt估计的影响，并提出了在结构化群体中使用单群体模型时选择世代间隔的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖的再生数Rt是追踪传染病传播和量化干预效果的重要指标。现有Rt推断模型通常假设所有人群的世代间隔分布相同，但现实中不同人群组间可能存在系统性差异。本研究旨在探讨这种差异对Rt估计的影响，并寻找解决方案。

Method: 研究采用两种基于更新方程的Rt推断框架：单群体同质模型和结构化群体模型。通过解析分析和模拟研究比较两种模型的估计差异，提出在结构化群体中使用单群体模型时选择世代间隔的方法，并使用真实流行病数据验证结果。

Result: 研究发现，当不同人群组的世代间隔分布存在差异时，单群体模型和结构化群体模型的Rt估计结果会不同。研究提出了一种选择世代间隔的方法，使得单群体模型能够在结构化群体中正确推断Rt。真实数据验证表明，两种模型的实践估计确实存在差异。

Conclusion: 群体间世代间隔分布的差异会影响Rt估计的准确性。为改进Rt估计以指导公共卫生政策，需要严格收集详细的流行病数据并考虑人群组间的差异。研究结果为在结构化群体中使用单群体模型提供了方法指导。

Abstract: Due to its ability to summarise 'real-time' epidemic behaviour, the time-dependent reproduction number, Rt, is a useful metric for tracking pathogen transmission and quantifying the effects of interventions during infectious disease outbreaks. The predominant models underlying inferred Rt trajectories are renewal equations, their success owing in part to the relatively few assumptions they require. One necessary assumption is the generation time distribution, which summarises the time periods between infections in infector-infectee transmission pairs. This distribution is typically assumed to be the same across all members of a population. In reality, however, it may vary systematically between population groups. In this study, we consider two Rt inference frameworks based on renewal equation models: one for a single, homogeneous group and another accounting for a structured population. We compare the estimates of Rt generated by the two models and investigate, both analytically and through simulations, under which conditions the conclusions drawn from these modelling paradigms differ. We also demonstrate a methodology for selecting the generation time for the one-group model that correctly encapsulates variations between different population groups; this allows us to use a renewal framework for a one-group model to infer Rt when, in fact, the population is structured. Finally, we use real epidemic data to demonstrate that practical Rt estimates can differ depending on whether the underlying model is the one-group model or the multi-group model. Our results motivate the need for rigorous collection of detailed epidemic data and consideration of differences between population groups to improve the accuracy of Rt estimates that are used to guide public health policy responses.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Szczarba's twisted shuffle and equivariant path homology of directed graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09406)
*Xin Fu,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在标记单纯集背景下，Szczarba扭曲洗牌映射限制为路径链复形上的链同构，并将此应用于带群作用的有向图，定义了等变路径同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带群作用的标记单纯集的路径同调理论，将经典的扭曲笛卡尔积理论推广到标记范畴，为有向图等结构的等变同调提供理论框架。

Method: 使用标记扭曲笛卡尔积（通过盒积定义），证明Szczarba扭曲洗牌映射在标记背景下限制为路径链复形上的链同构，并构造等变路径同调的自然Borel构造。

Result: 在标记单纯集背景下，Szczarba扭曲洗牌映射限制为路径链复形上的链同构；对有向图群作用，获得了作为标记扭曲笛卡尔积特例的自然Borel构造，等变路径同调可由显式扭曲张量积计算。

Conclusion: 成功将经典扭曲笛卡尔积理论推广到标记范畴，建立了等变路径同调的理论框架，为带群作用的有向图等结构提供了有效的同调计算方法。

Abstract: To a marked simplicial set one can associate its path chain complex, and define its homology to be the homology of this complex, inspired by path homology theories for directed graphs, quivers, and marked categories. Given a marked simplicial set with a simplicial group action preserving the markings and degenerate 1-simplices, together with a twisting function, we define a marked twisted Cartesian product using the box product.
  Classically, Szczarba's twisted shuffle provides a quasi-isomorphism between the chain complex of a twisted Cartesian product and the corresponding twisted tensor product. In this paper, we prove that in the marked setting, this map restricts to a chain isomorphism on path chain complexes. As an application, for directed graphs with group actions, we obtain a natural Borel construction as a special case of marked twisted Cartesian products. Equivariant path homology is defined as the homology of this construction and is computed by an explicit twisted tensor product.

</details>


### [127] [On the topological complexity of non-simply connected spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09407)
*Yuki Minowa*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文将拓扑复杂度的nilpotency下界理论扩展到群同态情形，并应用于确定某些非阿贝尔基本群的3-流形的拓扑复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非单连通空间的拓扑复杂度，特别是如何通过群同态扩展现有的nilpotency下界理论，以计算具有非阿贝尔基本群的3-流形的拓扑复杂度

Method: 将Costa-Farber的cohomology类nilpotency下界理论和Farber-Mescher的谱序列方法扩展到群同态情形，建立相应的理论框架

Result: 成功扩展了拓扑复杂度的nilpotency下界理论到群同态情形，并应用该理论确定了某些具有非阿贝尔基本群的3-流形的拓扑复杂度

Conclusion: 通过群同态扩展拓扑复杂度的nilpotency下界理论是有效的，为计算非单连通空间特别是具有复杂基本群的3-流形的拓扑复杂度提供了新工具

Abstract: Topological complexity is a numerical homotopy invariant that measures the instability of motion planning in a space. To study the topological complexity of non-simply connected spaces, Costa and Farber introduced a cohomology class whose nilpotency gives a lower bound of topological complexity. Farber and Mescher constructed a spectral sequence that evaluates this nilpotency without direct computation. We extend these results with respect to a group homomorphism. As an application, we determine the topological complexity of some 3-manifolds with nonabelian fundamental group.

</details>


### [128] [The homotopy type of the moment-angle complex associated to the complex of injective words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09491)
*Pedro Conceição*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何从有向图的组合数据构建拓扑空间，特别关注注入词复形的面偏序集上的矩角复形的同伦类型，揭示了同伦与组合学之间的紧密联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑方法已成为分析有向图结构特性（特别是计算神经科学中的连接组数据）的重要工具。研究通过多面体积函子从有向图的组合数据构建拓扑空间，旨在理解其同伦类型，这对拓扑学和组合学都有独立意义。

Method: 使用多面体积函子从有向图的组合数据构造拓扑空间。具体计算了注入词复形的面偏序集上的矩角复形的同伦类型。还构造了有序单纯复形相关多面体积的同伦纤维化，这推广了抽象单纯复形上多面体积的类似同伦纤维化。

Result: 揭示了同伦与组合学之间的紧密联系：矩角复形的同伦类型由注入词复形的h向量决定。构造了有序单纯复形相关多面体积的同伦纤维化，推广了现有理论。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了有向图组合数据与拓扑空间之间的桥梁，展示了注入词复形的组合特性如何决定相关矩角复形的同伦类型，为分析连接组数据等应用提供了新的拓扑工具，并推广了多面体积的同伦纤维化理论。

Abstract: Topological methods have emerged as valuable tools for analyzing the structural properties of directed graphs, particularly connectome data in computational neuroscience. This paper investigates the construction of topological spaces from combinatorial data of directed graphs using the polyhedral product functor, with particular emphasis on understanding their homotopy type, which is also of independent interest in topology and combinatorics. Specifically, we compute the homotopy type of the moment-angle complex over the face poset of the complex of injective words. This reveals a tight connection between homotopy and combinatorics: its homotopy type is determined by the $h$-vector of complexes of injective words. We also construct an associated homotopy fibration of polyhedral products associated to ordered simplicial complexes, which in a way generalizes the analogous homotopy fibration for polyhedral products over abstract simplicial complexes.

</details>


### [129] [Fractured Structures in Condensed Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09618)
*Nima Rasekh,Qi Zhu*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 在凝聚动画的∞-topos上构造了Lurie意义上的断裂结构，用于展示凝聚动画的联合保守点集，并分析了极不连通空间范畴的极限性质


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解凝聚动画的各种性质，特别是为了展示凝聚动画的显式联合保守点集，需要构造断裂结构。同时，为了排除其他可能的断裂结构候选，需要分析极不连通空间范畴的极限性质

Method: 在凝聚动画的∞-topos上构造了Lurie意义上的断裂结构，利用该结构展示了凝聚动画的显式联合保守点集。同时，通过分析极不连通空间范畴的极限性质，特别是证明该范畴不具有所有纤维，来排除其他可能的断裂结构候选

Result: 成功构造了凝聚动画∞-topos上的断裂结构，并利用该结构展示了凝聚动画的显式联合保守点集。证明了极不连通空间范畴不具有所有纤维，从而回答了Clausen提出的问题

Conclusion: 断裂结构的构造为理解凝聚动画的性质提供了新工具，特别是用于展示联合保守点集。对极不连通空间范畴极限性质的分析有助于排除其他可能的断裂结构候选，深化了对相关范畴结构的理解

Abstract: We construct a fractured structure, in the sense of Lurie, on the $\infty$-topos of condensed anima. This fractured structure allows us to better comprehend various properties of condensed anima - we use it to exhibit an explicit collection of jointly conservative points for condensed anima. To rule out further candidates for fractured structures, we analyze limits in the category of extremally disconnected spaces. In particular, we show that it does not admit all fibers, answering a question from Clausen.

</details>


### [130] [Homotopy Posets, Postnikov Towers, and Hypercompletions of $\infty$-Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09903)
*David Gepner,Hadrian Heine*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文将同伦理论的基本概念（如同伦集、同伦群、连通映射、截断映射、胞腔构造等）扩展到(∞,∞)-范畴的框架中，并研究了在Gray张量积下可表现范畴的相应理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究(∞,∞)-范畴的同伦理论，将经典同伦论的概念推广到更高维范畴框架中，探索定向范畴的独特性质及其与拓扑情况的差异。

Method: 通过引入"同伦偏序集"的概念，这些偏序集由范畴盘的边界索引；建立定向版本的纤维化长正合序列；构造范畴Postnikov塔；研究截断态射和连通态射的性质。

Result: 发现定向多面体等几何构造块虽然弱可缩但具有非平凡同伦偏序集；Postnikov塔对(∞,n)-范畴收敛但对一般(∞,∞)-范畴不收敛；通过反演共归纳等价得到Postnikov完备(∞,∞)-范畴的完全子范畴。

Conclusion: 成功建立了(∞,∞)-范畴的同伦理论框架，揭示了定向范畴与拓扑情况的本质差异，为高阶范畴理论提供了新的同伦工具和结构理解。

Abstract: We show that basic homotopical notions such as homotopy sets and groups, connected and truncated maps, cellular constructions and skeleta, etc., extend to the setting of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories, as well as to presentable categories enriched in $(\infty,\infty)$-categories under the Gray tensor product. The homotopy posets of an $(\infty,\infty)$-category are indexed by boundaries of categorical disks; in particular, there is a fundamental poset for each pair of objects, which we regard as a oriented point where the source and target objects have opposite orientation. In contrast to the situation in topology, weakly contractible geometric building blocks such as oriented polytopes typically have nontrivial homotopy posets. The homotopy posets assemble to form an oriented analogue of the long exact sequence of a fibration and form the layers of a categorical Postnikov tower, which converges for any $(\infty,n)$-category but not for general $(\infty,\infty)$-categories. We show that the full subcategory consisting of the Postnikov complete $(\infty,\infty)$-categories is obtained by inverting the coinductive equivalences and canonically identifies with the limit of the categories of $(\infty,n)$-categories taken along the truncation functors. We also study truncated morphisms in general oriented categories and connected morphisms in presentable oriented categories.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [A finite element continuous data assimilation framework for a Navier--Stokes--Cahn--Hilliard system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08765)
*Tianyu Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文为耦合的Navier-Stokes-Cahn-Hilliard相场模型开发了连续数据同化框架，通过粗粒度空间观测恢复轨迹，建立了数值格式并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何从粗粒度空间观测中恢复复杂多相流系统的精细尺度演化轨迹，解决相场模型中数据同化的理论和方法问题。

Method: 提出基于nudging的连续数据同化系统，使用带截断的完全离散有限元分裂格式，采用连续二次元表示相、化学势、速度和辅助场，连续线性元表示压力。

Result: 证明了数值格式的单步适定性，建立了截断方法的逐步先验估计，数值实验展示了从强不匹配初始条件恢复的能力，分析了同步对观测分辨率、边界强迫和反馈强度的依赖性。

Conclusion: 所提出的CDA框架能够有效从粗粒度观测中恢复精细尺度轨迹，揭示了粗粒度不可区分性现象，同化动力学能够选择由时间相关观测确定的轨迹。

Abstract: This paper studies a coupled two-dimensional Navier--Stokes--Cahn--Hilliard phase-field model augmented by a transported auxiliary field, and develops a continuous data assimilation (CDA) framework for recovering its trajectories from coarse-in-space observations.
  We formulate a nudging-based CDA system for the coupled NSCH--auxiliary-field model, in which coarse measurements are incorporated through a general linear observation operator. The observation mechanism is described abstractly by an interpolant satisfying an $H^{2}$-type approximation property, which is compatible with coarse spatial observations obtained from mesh coarsening and reconstruction.
  At the continuous level, we record two structural properties of the model: a formal energy law for the reference system and an evolution law for the phase mean in the assimilated dynamics. At the discrete level, we introduce a capped fully discrete finite element splitting scheme using continuous quadratic elements for the phase, chemical potential, velocity, and auxiliary field, together with continuous linear elements for the pressure. For this scheme, we prove one-step well-posedness and establish a stepwise a priori estimate for the capped method.
  Numerical experiments illustrate the practical behavior of the proposed CDA approach. They demonstrate recovery from strongly mismatched initial conditions and show how synchronization depends on the observation resolution, boundary forcing, and feedback strength. A coarse-indistinguishability experiment further shows that identical coarse initial information may correspond to distinct fine-scale evolutions, while the assimilated dynamics select the trajectory determined by the supplied time-dependent observations.

</details>


### [132] [An efficient predictor-corrector approach with orthogonal spline collocation finite element technique for FitzHugh-Nagumo problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08772)
*Eric Ngondiep*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于模拟FitzHugh-Nagumo系统的预测-校正算法，结合正交样条配置有限元方法，具有无条件稳定性、高精度和计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种高效、稳定且高精度的数值方法来模拟FitzHugh-Nagumo系统，克服传统方法中可能出现的数值振荡、稳定性问题和计算复杂度高的挑战

Method: 采用预测-校正技术：预测阶段使用变时间步长，校正阶段使用固定时间步长；空间离散采用正交样条配置有限元方法；对非线性项进行线性化处理以减少计算量

Result: 新算法具有无条件稳定性、空间m阶精度和时间二阶收敛性；能有效处理奇异性问题；数值实验验证了理论分析，展示了算法的适用性和性能

Conclusion: 提出的预测-校正正交样条配置有限元方法为FitzHugh-Nagumo系统提供了一种高效、稳定且高精度的数值模拟工具，特别适用于存在奇异性的情况

Abstract: This paper constructs a predictor-corrector technique with orthogonal spline collocation finite element method for simulating a FitzHugh-Nagumo system subject to suitable initial and boundary conditions. The developed computational technique approximates the exact solution in time using variable time steps at the predictor phase and a constant time step at the corrector stage while the orthogonal spline collocation finite element method is employed in the space discretization. The new algorithm presents several advantages: (i) the errors increased at the predictor phase are balanced by the ones decreased at the corrector phase so that the stability is preserved, (ii) the variable time steps at the predictor stage overcome the numerical oscillations, (iii) the spatial errors are minimized due to the use of collocation nodes, and (iv) the linearization of the nonlinear term reduces the required operations at the corrector stage. As a result, the new computational technique computes efficiently both predicted and corrected solutions and preserves a strong stability and high-order accuracy even in the presence of singularities. The theoretical results suggest that the constructed approach is unconditionally stable, spatial $mth$-order accurate and temporal second-order convergent in the $L^{\infty}(0,T;[H^{m}(Ω)]^{2})$-norm. Some numerical experiments are carried out to confirm the theoretical analysis and to demonstrate the applicability and performance of the proposed strategy.

</details>


### [133] [On $p$-robust convergence and optimality of adaptive FEM driven by equilibrated-flux estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08887)
*Théophile Chaumont-Frelet,Zhaonan Dong,Gregor Gantner,Martin Vohralík*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于平衡通量估计子的h自适应算法，用于固定多项式阶p的有限元离散，证明算法在满足可验证后验准则时具有与p无关的误差收缩因子，并在Dörfler标记参数低于特定阈值时以最优代数速率收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有hp自适应算法已取得进展，但需要开发专门针对固定多项式阶p的h自适应算法，以实现在满足特定条件下与p无关的误差收缩和最优收敛速率，同时保持p鲁棒性。

Method: 基于平衡通量估计子构建h自适应算法，采用符合有限元离散泊松方程，假设分段多项式右端项，通过Dörfler标记策略和最新顶点二分法进行局部网格细化。

Result: 算法在满足可验证后验准则时，每一步都能实现误差收缩，收缩因子与p无关；当Dörfler标记参数低于特定p无关上界时，算法以最优代数速率s收敛，相关常数具有p鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的h自适应算法在理论分析和数值实验中均表现出良好性能，后验准则在实际计算中通常只需一到几次局部网格细化即可满足，验证了算法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Building on existing $hp$-adaptive algorithms driven by equilibrated-flux estimators from [ESAIM Math. Model. Numer. Anal. 57 (2023), 329--366] and the references therein, we propose a novel $h$-adaptive algorithm for a fixed polynomial degree $p$. We consider a conforming finite element discretization of the Poisson equation in two or three space dimensions. Supposing piecewise polynomial right-hand side, we show that the algorithm yields error contraction at each step, with a contraction factor that is independent of $p$ provided that a certain {\sl a posteriori} verifiable criterion is satisfied. We further show that this algorithm converges at optimal algebraic rate $s$ if the Dörfler marking parameter is chosen below some specified $p$-independent upper threshold. The constants involved here are $p$-robust, although they may depend on the rate $s$. The theoretical results are supported by numerical experiments, in which the {\sl a posteriori} criterion is always satisfied for one or a few local mesh refinement steps by newest-vertex bisection.

</details>


### [134] [Overlapping Schwarz Preconditioners for Pose-Graph SLAM in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08975)
*Stephan Köhler,Oliver Rheinbach,Yue Xiang Tee,Sebastian Zug*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 论文研究了在机器人SLAM的位姿图优化中，使用加性重叠Schwarz域分解方法作为图基非线性最小二乘问题线性系统的预处理器，证明了其数值可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人SLAM中的位姿图优化会产生大型稀疏线性系统，传统求解方法在问题规模增大时效率下降。需要开发可扩展的预处理器来加速求解过程。

Method: 采用加性重叠Schwarz域分解方法作为预处理器，结合预处理共轭梯度法求解高斯-牛顿线性化产生的线性系统。将简化SLAM问题解释为使用线性弹性杆的有限元问题，建立与PDE离散化的结构类比。

Result: 数值实验表明，使用加性重叠Schwarz预处理器时，预处理共轭梯度法的迭代次数与问题规模无关，证明了方法的数值可扩展性。

Conclusion: 域分解预处理器能有效处理SLAM位姿图优化中的大规模线性系统，基于PDE的预处理器适用于图优化问题，为机器人SLAM后端优化提供了可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: We investigate the application of the additive overlapping Schwarz domain decomposition method as a preconditioner for the large sparse linear systems arising in graph-based nonlinear least-squares problems, specifically the pose-graph optimization back-end in Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) in robotics. A brief introduction to both SLAM and domain decomposition preconditioners is given, followed by a description of the nonlinear least-squares formulation, its linearization, and the resulting matrix structure, making the paper accessible to readers without prior knowledge of either field. Numerical experiments for a simple model problem demonstrate the numerical scalability of the preconditioned conjugate gradient method to solve the linear systems resulting from Gauss--Newton linearization: Using the additive overlapping Schwarz preconditioner, the number of conjugate gradient iterations remains bounded independently of the problem size. We also show that a simplified SLAM problem can be interpreted as a finite element problem using linear elastic bars, highlighting the structural analogy to PDE discretizations and motivating the use of PDE-based preconditioners such as scalable domain decomposition preconditioners.

</details>


### [135] [Four-field mixed finite elements for incompressible nonlinear elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08992)
*Santiago Badia,Wei Li,Ricardo Ruiz-Baier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于四场混合公式的稳定有限元方法，用于不可压缩非线性弹性力学，采用间断位移场且无需稳定化处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四场混合公式（如CSFEM）通常需要稳定化处理，特别是在3D情况下。本文旨在开发一种无需稳定化的稳定有限元方法，提高计算鲁棒性。

Method: 采用包含位移、位移梯度、第一Piola-Kirchhoff应力和压力的四场混合公式，使用间断位移场，无需稳定化。推导Newton-Raphson线性化，识别满足inf-sup条件的有限元对，并引入高效后处理技术恢复连续位移场。

Result: 建立了线性化连续问题的适定性，给出了离散格式的先验误差估计。2D和3D数值实验显示最优甚至超收敛率，鲁棒性增强，特别是在3D中CSFEM通常需要稳定化的场景下表现优异。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需稳定化的稳定四场混合有限元方法，在不可压缩非线性弹性力学中实现了高精度和强鲁棒性，特别是在3D问题中优于现有方法。

Abstract: We present a stable finite element method for incompressible nonlinear elasticity based on a four-field mixed formulation involving the displacement, displacement gradient, first Piola--Kirchhoff stress and pressure. Unlike existing four-field mixed formulations, such as the compatible strain mixed finite element method (CSFEM), the proposed approach employs a discontinuous displacement field and requires no stabilisation in either 2D or 3D. A Newton--Raphson linearisation is derived and finite element pairs satisfying the relevant inf-sup conditions are identified. To recover accurate continuous displacement fields, an efficient postprocessing technique is further introduced. We establish the well-posedness of the linearised continuous problem together with a priori error estimates for the discrete formulation. Extensive numerical experiments in both 2D and 3D demonstrate optimal or even super convergence rates and enhanced robustness, particularly in 3D where CSFEM typically requires stabilisation.

</details>


### [136] [A direct sampling method for inverse time-dependent electromagnetic source problems: reconstruction of the radiating time and spatial support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09143)
*Fenglin Sun,Hongxia Guo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于重构时变电磁波源时空特征的新型直接采样方法，利用多频远场测量数据同时恢复源的辐射时间和空间支撑


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样方法主要关注重构源的空间支撑，缺乏同时重构时空特征的能力。时变电磁波逆源问题在傅里叶变换后形成频域系统，需要处理频率相关的源项，这为同时恢复时空信息提供了可能性。

Method: 通过傅里叶变换将时域问题转为频域系统，提出直接采样方法：1）利用相反观测方向的多频数据对获取辐射时间；2）用单一观测方向的多频数据重构包含源支撑的最小超平面区域；3）利用稀疏观测方向的多频数据重构源支撑的Theta凸包。

Result: 方法能够同时重构源的时空特征：确定辐射时间、包含源支撑的最小超平面区域以及源支撑的Theta凸包。三维数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的直接采样方法成功解决了时变电磁波逆源问题中同时重构时空特征的挑战，相比现有方法具有显著优势，为电磁波源定位和特征提取提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates inverse source problems for time-dependent electromagnetic waves governed by Maxwell's equations. After applying the Fourier transform with respect to time, the problem leads to a frequency-domain electromagnetic system with a frequency-dependent source term. We propose a novel direct sampling method for reconstructing such radiating time and spatial space of sources from multi-frequency far-field measurements. By using a pair of multi-frequency data from opposite observation directions, we can obtain the radiating time of the signal. Based on this, the smallest region between two hyperplanes containing the support of source can be reconstructed using multi frequency data from one observation direction. The Theta convex hull of the source support can be reconstructed from multi-frequency data from sparse observation directions. Compared with existing sampling methods that mainly focus on reconstructing the spatial support, the proposed approach allows for the simultaneous reconstruction of both spatial and temporal features of the source.Three-dimensional numerical examples are conducted to validate the effectiveness of the algorithm.

</details>


### [137] [Exponential Convergence of $hp$-FEM for the Integral Fractional Laplacian on cuboids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09449)
*Björn Bahr,Markus Faustmann,Carlo Marcati,Jens Markus Melenk,Christoph Schwab*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对Dirichlet积分分数阶Laplacian，证明了在(0,1)^3上张量积hp有限元近似的根指数收敛性，对解析右端项f，获得能量范数误差界≲exp(-b∛N)


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分数阶Laplacian问题的数值解法，特别是hp有限元方法在三维立方体域上的收敛性分析，旨在为这类具有奇异性的问题提供高效的数值逼近理论

Method: 采用张量积hp有限元方法，利用加权Sobolev空间中的解析正则性估计，构建向(0,1)^3所有边界几何细化的网格族，使用hp-GLL插值近似和hp-Galerkin有限元方法

Result: 证明了hp-GLL插值近似在N个自由度下的能量范数误差界为≲exp(-b∛N)，数值实验验证了这一理论界

Conclusion: 对于Dirichlet积分分数阶Laplacian问题，在三维立方体域上，当右端项解析时，张量积hp有限元方法能够实现根指数收敛，为这类问题的数值求解提供了理论保证

Abstract: For the Dirichlet integral fractional Laplacian, we prove root exponential convergence of tensor-product $hp$-finite element approximations on $(0,1)^3$, for forcing $f$ that is analytic in $[0,1]^3$. Exploiting analytic regularity estimates in weighted Sobolev spaces, we prove for $hp$-GLL interpolation approximations with $N$ degrees of freedom the energy norm error bound $\lesssim \exp(-b\sqrt[6]{N})$. Tensor product mesh families which are geometrically refined towards all sides of $(0,1)^3$ are used. Numerical experiments with $hp$-Galerkin FEM confirm the bound.

</details>


### [138] [Scalable s-step Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient with Chebyshev Basis and Gauss-Seidel Gram Solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09790)
*Pasqua D'Ambra,Massimo Bernaschi,Mauro G. Carrozzo,Stephen Thomas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种结合Chebyshev稳定Krylov基和正向Gauss-Seidel迭代的s步预条件共轭梯度变体，用于解决Gram系统，减少同步开销同时保持稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s步共轭梯度方法通过每次外迭代生成多个搜索方向来减少全局同步成本，但需要求解小型密集Gram系统，其条件数对稳定性至关重要。传统方法在稳定性与效率之间存在权衡。

Method: 1) 使用Chebyshev稳定化Krylov基改善Gram矩阵条件数；2) 分析Chebyshev Gram矩阵结构，证明其基于矩的表示对中等步长具有有利条件数特性；3) 结合正向Gauss-Seidel迭代求解约简Gram系统；4) 基于不精确Krylov理论和FGS与MGS的经典等价性，提供理论分析支持少量FGS扫描。

Result: 1) 理论分析表明Chebyshev Gram矩阵对中等步长具有良好条件数；2) 少量FGS扫描即可保持收敛行为；3) 在NVIDIA GPU架构上的大规模实验显示，该方法达到与传统CG相当的收敛性，同时显著减少同步开销。

Conclusion: 提出的Chebyshev稳定化、Gauss-Seidel增强的s步PCG方法为当前和下一代加速器系统提供了稳定且可扩展的替代方案，在保持收敛性的同时有效减少同步开销。

Abstract: We present a variant of the s-step Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient (PCG) method that combines a Chebyshev-stabilized Krylov basis with a Forward Gauss-Seidel (FGS) iteration for the solution of the reduced Gram systems. In s-step Conjugate Gradient, multiple search directions are generated per outer iteration, reducing global synchronization costs but requiring the solution of small dense Gram systems whose conditioning is critical for stability. We analyze the structure of the Chebyshev Gram matrix and show that its moment-based representation is associated with favorable conditioning properties for moderate step sizes. Building on inexact Krylov theory and on the classical equivalence between FGS and Modified Gram-Schmidt (MGS), we provide a structural analysis and theoretical rationale supporting the use of a small number of FGS sweeps, while preserving the convergence behavior observed in practical regimes. Large-scale experiments on modern NVIDIA GPU architectures demonstrate that the proposed Chebyshev-stabilized, Gauss-Seidel-enhanced s-step PCG achieves convergence comparable to classical CG while reducing synchronization overhead, making it a stable and scalable alternative for current and next-generation accelerator systems.

</details>


### [139] [Subspace decomposition with defect diffusion coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09924)
*Dilini Kolombage,Axel Målqvist,Barbara Verfürth*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于椭圆扩散问题的离线-在线子空间分解预处理器，通过预计算参考配置的局部子空间解来加速不确定性量化中的蒙特卡洛模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度异质系数椭圆扩散问题导致离散系统条件数差，需要有效预处理器。传统子空间分解预处理器对固定系数实现效果好，但在不确定性量化（特别是蒙特卡洛模拟）中，对大量细尺度实现重复构建预处理器计算成本过高。

Method: 提出离线-在线子空间分解预处理器近似方法，利用随机缺陷的局部化结构。离线阶段预计算少量参考配置的局部子空间解；在线阶段将这些预计算结果高效组合用于任意实现。

Result: 分析了离线-在线近似算子的谱性质，通过数值实验证实了该预处理器的鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持子空间分解预处理器性能的同时，显著降低了不确定性量化中蒙特卡洛模拟的计算成本，为多尺度异质系数椭圆扩散问题提供了高效的求解策略。

Abstract: Elliptic diffusion problems with multiscale heterogeneous coefficients lead to poorly conditioned discrete systems and therefore require effective preconditioning strategies. While subspace decomposition preconditioners perform well for fixed realizations of the coefficient, their repeated construction becomes prohibitively expensive in uncertainty quantification settings, particularly in Monte-Carlo simulations, where a large number of fine-scale realizations must be treated. In this study, we propose an offline-online approximation of a subspace decomposition preconditioner that exploits the localized structure of the random defects. The preconditioner is constructed from local subspace solves that are precomputed offline for a small set of reference configurations and efficiently combined online for arbitrary realizations. We analyze the spectral properties of the resulting offline-online approximation operator and confirm its robustness and efficiency through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [140] [Understanding and Resolving Singularities in 3D Dirichlet Boundary Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09926)
*David Levin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段逼近方法，用于解决三维调和Dirichlet问题中的奇异性，通过将解分解为奇异部分和正则部分分别处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维调和Dirichlet问题中存在的奇异性给数值求解带来困难，需要开发能够有效处理这些奇异性的数值方法。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段使用格林函数表示，将解分解为奇异部分和正则部分；奇异阶段利用格林公式处理奇异部分，正则阶段引入光滑修正恢复正则部分；第一阶段采用高阶求积规则，第二阶段使用调和基函数进行配置。

Result: 该方法能够有效处理三维调和Dirichlet问题中的奇异性，通过奇异部分诱导必要的奇异性，正则部分提供光滑修正，从而获得完整的解。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段逼近方法为处理三维调和Dirichlet问题中的奇异性提供了一种有效的数值框架，结合了奇异分解和高阶数值技术。

Abstract: We introduce a two-phase approximation method designed to resolve singularities in three-dimensional harmonic Dirichlet problems. The approach utilizes the classical Green's function representation, decomposing the function into its singular and regular components.
  The singular phase employs Green's formula with the singular part, for which we show that it induces the necessary singularities in the solution. The regular phase then introduces a smooth correction to recover the remaining regular part of the solution. The construction employs high-order quadrature rules in the first phase, followed by collocation with a suitable harmonic basis in the second.

</details>


### [141] [Anchor-Based Function Extrapolation with Proven Bounds and Projection Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09937)
*Guy Hay,Nir Sharon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关的外推框架，将外推问题转化为可行性问题和投影问题，通过锚函数构建包含真实函数的可行集，并将基线近似投影到该集合以获得改进的外推预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近似和学习方法通常针对采样域内的插值进行优化，无法保证在外推区域的行为，小域内误差可能在外推区域被放大。需要开发具有严格理论保证的外推方法。

Method: 1) 使用锚函数构建辅助构造，可证明其与未知目标函数在外推区域距离的上界；2) 这些证明定义了包含真实函数的可行集；3) 将任何基线近似（如最小二乘或正则化回归）投影到可行集上，获得修正的外推预测。

Result: 1) 证明修正预测不会增加外推区域误差，并给出改进的定量界限；2) 建立了新的外推稳定性常数，包括紧谱条件数和数值稳定的内域界限；3) 提出概率锚函数以减少最坏情况证明的保守性；4) 数值实验（地磁场建模和非线性振荡器）显示外推误差显著降低。

Conclusion: 该框架为外推问题提供了模型无关的严格理论保证，通过锚函数和投影方法显著改善了外推性能，建立了外推稳定性分析的新理论工具，并通过概率方法减少了保守性。

Abstract: Classical approximation and learning methods are typically optimized for interpolation over a sampled domain Ω, with no guarantees on their behavior in an extrapolation region Ξ, where small in-domain errors may amplify. We develop a model-agnostic framework that recasts extrapolation as a feasibility and projection problem with rigorous guarantees. The approach is built around anchor functions, auxiliary constructions for which one can certify an upper bound on the Ξ-distance to the unknown target function. Such certificates define feasible sets that are proven to contain the true function. Given any baseline approximation (e.g., least-squares or regularized regression), we obtain a corrected extrapolation by projecting the baseline onto the feasible set; the resulting predictor is proven not to increase the error on Ξ, and we prove quantitative bounds on the improvement. We establish new stability constants governing extrapolation, including a tight spectral condition number and a numerically stable inner-domain bound that connects in-domain error to extrapolation risk. To reduce conservatism of worst-case certification, we also propose probabilistic anchor functions that yield high-confidence feasible sets. Numerical experiments, including geomagnetic field modeling and nonlinear oscillators, demonstrate substantial reductions in extrapolation error and corroborate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Structure and Representation Theory of basic simple $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$-graded color Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08986)
*Spyridon Afentoulidis-Almpanis*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文将复半单李代数的根理论方法推广到一类简单的ℤ₂×ℤ₂-分级（颜色）李代数，称为基本代数，并在嘉当子代数自中心化的假设下，通过证明最高权定理和完全可约性定理，分类了这些代数的所有有限维表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典李代数理论中的根理论方法扩展到更一般的ℤ₂×ℤ₂-分级（颜色）李代数，这类代数在数学物理中具有重要应用，但缺乏系统的表示论框架。

Method: 采用复半单李代数的理论方法，为基本ℤ₂×ℤ₂-分级李代数发展根理论，假设嘉当子代数自中心化，证明最高权定理和完全可约性定理。

Result: 成功建立了基本ℤ₂×ℤ₂-分级李代数的根理论，并在嘉当子代数自中心化的条件下，完全分类了所有有限维表示，证明了最高权定理和完全可约性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典李代数表示论推广到ℤ₂×ℤ₂-分级李代数，为这类代数提供了系统的表示理论框架，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We adapt methods from the theory of complex semisimple Lie algebras to develop a root theory for a class of simple $\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2$-graded (color) Lie algebras, which we call basic. As an application, assuming that the Cartan subalgebra is self-centralizing, we classify all finite-dimensional representations of these algebras by proving a highest weight theorem and a complete reducibility theorem.

</details>


### [143] [Algebraicity of the Brascamp-Lieb constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09057)
*Calin Chindris,Harm Derksen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明了Brascamp-Lieb常数是半代数函数，满足多项式关系P(V, BL(V,p))=0


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Brascamp-Lieb常数的代数性质，探索其在可行数据集上的函数特性

Method: 将问题推广到二部箭图表示相关的quiver BL常数框架，采用半代数函数理论分析

Result: 证明BL常数是半代数函数，满足多项式关系P(V, BL(V,p))=0，且在更一般的quiver BL常数设置中成立

Conclusion: Brascamp-Lieb常数具有代数性质，为相关数学领域提供了新的理论工具

Abstract: We show that the Brascamp-Lieb (BL) constant BL(-,p) is a semi-algebraic function on the set of feasible data. Consequently, it is algebraic in the sense that it satisfies a polynomial relation of the form P(V, BL(V,p))=0 for a non-zero polynomial P. In fact, we establish an analogous statement in the more general setting of quiver BL constants associated to representations of bipartite quivers.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Quantifying Uncertainty in AI Visibility: A Statistical Framework for Generative Search Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08924)
*Ronald Sielinski*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文指出AI答案引擎的引用具有随机性，传统单次测量方法会误导性地呈现精确结果，应使用置信区间和不确定性估计来评估领域可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式搜索的领域可见性测量通常依赖单次运行的引用份额和流行度点估计，将其视为固定值，但AI答案引擎本质上是非确定性的，相同查询在不同时间会产生不同响应和引用来源。这种随机性使得传统测量方法存在误导性。

Method: 对三个生成式搜索平台（Perplexity Search、OpenAI SearchGPT、Google Gemini）进行实证研究，使用三个消费产品主题的重复采样。采用两种采样机制：九天的每日收集和十分钟间隔的高频采样。分析引用分布形式，使用自助法置信区间评估差异显著性，并进行分布范围的排名稳定性分析。

Result: 引用分布遵循幂律形式，在重复采样中表现出显著变异性。自助法置信区间显示许多领域间的明显差异处于测量过程的噪声范围内。排名稳定性分析表明引用排名在整个频繁引用领域集中都不稳定，不仅限于顶级领域。单次运行的可见性度量提供了误导性的精确画面。

Conclusion: 引用可见性指标应被视为基础响应分布的样本估计量而非固定值。必须报告带有不确定性估计的引用可见性，论文提供了实现可解释置信区间所需的样本量实用指导。单次测量方法无法准确反映生成式搜索中的领域表现。

Abstract: AI-powered answer engines are inherently non-deterministic: identical queries submitted at different times can produce different responses and cite different sources. Despite this stochastic behavior, current approaches to measuring domain visibility in generative search typically rely on single-run point estimates of citation share and prevalence, implicitly treating them as fixed values. This paper argues that citation visibility metrics should be treated as sample estimators of an underlying response distribution rather than fixed values. We conduct an empirical study of citation variability across three generative search platforms--Perplexity Search, OpenAI SearchGPT, and Google Gemini--using repeated sampling across three consumer product topics. Two sampling regimes are employed: daily collections over nine days and high-frequency sampling at ten-minute intervals. We show that citation distributions follow a power-law form and exhibit substantial variability across repeated samples. Bootstrap confidence intervals reveal that many apparent differences between domains fall within the noise floor of the measurement process. Distribution-wide rank stability analysis further demonstrates that citation rankings are unstable across samples, not only among top-ranked domains but throughout the frequently cited domain set. These findings demonstrate that single-run visibility metrics provide a misleadingly precise picture of domain performance in generative search. We argue that citation visibility must be reported with uncertainty estimates and provide practical guidance for sample sizes required to achieve interpretable confidence intervals.

</details>


### [145] [Distribution-free screening of spatially variable genes in spatial transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09061)
*Changhu Wang,Qiyun Huang,Zihao Chen,Jin Liu,Ruibin Xi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出MM-test方法，基于准似然比统计量结合knockoff程序控制FDR，用于空间转录组数据中空间可变基因的筛选，特别适用于3D多切片数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组数据具有超高维度，识别与未知空间聚类相关的空间可变基因是核心任务，现有方法在处理3D数据和提供理论保证方面存在不足

Method: 开发基于准似然比统计量MM-test的分布自由SVG筛选方法，结合knockoff程序控制FDR，利用空间距离等辅助信息，适用于2D和3D多切片ST数据集

Result: 在模拟和34个真实ST数据集上的广泛基准测试表明，MM-test始终优于现有SVG检测方法；在3D小鼠脑数据中准确描绘了其他方法难以识别的精细结构

Conclusion: MM-test是一种高效、理论保证的SVG筛选方法，特别适用于3D空间转录组数据分析，为复杂空间结构的解析提供了有力工具

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) technologies enable transcriptome-wide gene expression profiling while preserving spatial resolution, offering unprecedented opportunities to uncover complex spatial structures. Due to the ultra-high dimensionality of ST data, identifying spatially variable genes (SVGs) associated with unknown spatial clusters has become a central task in ST data analysis. Here, we develop a distribution-free SVG screening method based on a novel quasi-likelihood ratio statistic, the MM-test, combined with a knockoff procedure to control the false discovery rate (FDR). MM-test leverages auxiliary information, such as spatial distances, about the unknown spatial domains for SVG screening. Notably, in addition to two-dimensional ST datasets, MM-test is well-suited for increasingly common three-dimensional (3D), multi-slice ST datasets. Extensive benchmarking using simulations and 34 real ST datasets demonstrates that MM-test consistently outperforms existing SVG detection methods. In a 3D mouse brain dataset, MM-test accurately delineates fine-scale structures that are challenging for other methods, such as the 3D architecture of the pyramidal layer of the hippocampal cornu ammonis and the dentate gyrus. Theoretical guarantees-including selection consistency, FDR control, and an error bound for post-selection clustering-are also established.

</details>


### [146] [CERES: A Probabilistic Early Warning System for Acute Food Insecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09425)
*Tom Danny S. Pedersen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: CERES是一个自动化的概率性急性粮食不安全预测系统，为43个高风险国家生成90天前IPC第3+、4+和5阶段的概率估计，每周更新，融合六大数据流并通过逻辑评分模型进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要建立一个同时具备概率性、开放访问、持续运行、机器可读预测级别并承诺公开前瞻性验证的饥荒早期预警系统，以改进急性粮食不安全的预测能力。

Method: 系统融合六大数据流：降水异常(CHIRPS)、植被指数(MODIS NDVI)、冲突事件(ACLED)、IPC分类、粮食消费评分(WFP)和谷物价格指数(FAO/WFP)，通过逻辑评分模型进行处理，使用作者指定的初始系数和参数化输入扰动区间(n=2,000次抽样)。

Result: 在针对四个IPC第4-5阶段事件的历史回测验证中，CERES在所有四个案例中都分配了TIER-1分类；所有前瞻性预测都带有时间戳、加密标识并归档，供在T+90时间点与IPC结果数据进行公开验证。

Conclusion: CERES是首个同时具备概率性、开放访问、持续运行、机器可读预测级别并承诺公开前瞻性验证的饥荒早期预警系统，为急性粮食不安全预测提供了创新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present CERES (Calibrated Early-warning and Risk Estimation System), an automated probabilistic forecasting system for acute food insecurity. CERES generates 90-day ahead probability estimates of IPC Phase 3+ (Crisis), Phase 4+ (Emergency), and Phase 5 (Famine) conditions for 43 high-risk countries globally, updated weekly. The system fuses six data streams, precipitation anomalies (CHIRPS), vegetation indices (MODIS NDVI), conflict events (ACLED), IPC classifications, food consumption scores (WFP), and cereal price indices (FAO/WFP) - through a logistic scoring model with author-specified initial coefficients and parametric input-perturbation intervals (n=2,000 draws). In historical back-validation against four IPC Phase 4-5 events selected for data completeness, CERES assigned TIER-1 classification in all four cases; these are in-sample sanity checks only, not prospective performance claims. All prospective predictions are timestamped, cryptographically identified, and archived for public verification against IPC outcome data at the T+90 horizon. To the author's knowledge, CERES is the first famine early warning system that is simultaneously: (1) probabilistic, (2) open-access, (3) continuously running, (4) machine-readable at prediction level, and (5) committed to public prospective verification of every prediction made.

</details>


### [147] [Bayesian Species Distribution Models using Hierarchical Decomposition Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09428)
*Luisa Ferrari,Massimo Ventrucci,Alex Laini*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 将分层分解先验框架应用于贝叶斯物种分布模型，实现方差分量的直接透明先验控制，提高可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态学中需要理解环境、空间和时间过程对物种分布的相对贡献，但贝叶斯物种分布模型中方差分量的先验设定具有挑战性

Method: 将分层分解先验框架适配到潜高斯物种分布模型，通过分解树将方差重新参数化为总方差和可解释的比例，反映模型结构和生态学意义分组

Result: 使用NOAA东北渔业科学中心秋季底拖网调查中39种底栖鱼类的存在-缺失数据进行验证，预测性能与现有先验相当，但方差归因和先验敏感性分析的可解释性和透明度显著提高

Conclusion: 分层分解先验框架为贝叶斯物种分布模型提供了更透明、可解释的方差分量先验设定方法，有助于生态学中的方差分解分析

Abstract: Understanding the relative contributions of environmental, spatial, and temporal processes in shaping species distribution is a central objective in ecology. Bayesian species distribution models (SDMs) offer a flexible framework for this task, yet prior specification for variance components remains challenging. To address this issue, we adapt the Hierarchical Decomposition (HD) prior framework to latent Gaussian SDMs, enabling direct and transparent prior control over variance partitioning. The HD approach reparametrizes variances into a total variance and a set of interpretable proportions, structured through a decomposition tree that reflects both model architecture and ecologically meaningful groupings of effects. We discuss a principled approach for a default tree design tailored to SDMs and a practical workflow for the step-by-step implementation of the method. The framework is illustrated using presence--absence data for 39 demersal fish species from the NOAA Northeast Fisheries Science Center fall bottom trawl survey. Results demonstrate predictive performance comparable to established priors, while providing substantially improved interpretability and transparency in variance attribution and prior sensitivity analysis.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Spectral transitions in some Rabi models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08834)
*Grzegorz Świderski,Lech Zieliński*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 使用从属理论分析量子Rabi模型从离散谱到连续谱的转变，证明三类模型中本质谱的存在性、无奇异谱以及本质谱内部无特征值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子Rabi模型中离散谱向连续谱的转变现象，特别是强度依赖Rabi模型、各向异性双光子Rabi模型和双光子Rabi-Stark模型在整个参数范围内的谱性质

Method: 采用从属理论（subordinacy theory）来检测和定位三类模型的本质谱，该方法适用于分析微分算子的谱性质

Result: 证明了所有考虑模型中本质谱的存在性，确认了奇异谱的缺失，并证明了本质谱内部不存在特征值

Conclusion: 从属理论为分析量子Rabi模型的谱结构提供了有效工具，三类模型在整个参数范围内都表现出相似的谱性质：存在本质谱、无奇异谱、本质谱内部无特征值

Abstract: We consider the transition from discrete to continuous spectrum which occurs in some quantum Rabi models. The subordinacy theory is used to detect and locate the essential spectrum of the intensity-dependent Rabi model, the anisotropic two-photon Rabi model, and the two-photon Rabi-Stark model for the whole range of parameters. The absence of a singular spectrum and the absence of eigenvalues in the interior of the essential spectrum are proved for all models considered.

</details>


### [149] [State integral models and the tetrahedron equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09230)
*Junya Yagi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类状态积分模型（包括Teichmüller TQFT的边缘表述）中，分配给四面体的玻尔兹曼权重满足四面体方程，其中四面体的二面角扮演谱参数的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究形状伪3-流形上的状态积分模型，特别是Teichmüller拓扑量子场理论（TQFT）的边缘表述，旨在理解这些模型中四面体权重与四面体方程之间的关系，探索几何参数（二面角）如何作为谱参数出现在量子可积系统中。

Method: 作者分析了一类状态积分模型，重点关注Teichmüller TQFT的边缘表述。通过数学推导证明，在这些模型中分配给四面体的玻尔兹曼权重满足四面体方程（一种三维可积系统的Yang-Baxter型方程），并将四面体的几何参数（二面角）识别为谱参数。

Result: 主要结果是：对于所研究的形状伪3-流形上的状态积分模型，特别是Teichmüller TQFT的边缘表述，四面体的玻尔兹曼权重确实满足四面体方程。这一发现建立了这些拓扑量子场理论与三维可积系统之间的直接联系，其中四面体的几何结构自然地提供了谱参数。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了Teichmüller TQFT等状态积分模型与三维可积系统之间的深刻联系，证明了几何参数（二面角）可以作为谱参数出现在四面体方程中。这一结果为理解拓扑量子场理论与可积系统之间的关系提供了新的视角，并可能促进这两个领域的进一步交叉研究。

Abstract: It is shown that for a class of state integral models on shaped pseudo 3-manifolds, including the edge formulation of Teichmüller TQFT, the Boltzmann weight assigned to a tetrahedron solves the tetrahedron equation. The dihedral angles of the tetrahedron play the role of spectral parameters.

</details>


### [150] [On Fermi's model for the scattering of a slow neutron from a bound proton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09372)
*Domenico Finco,Raffaele Scandone,Alessandro Teta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究费米1936年提出的中子-质子双粒子系统哈密顿量，其中中子与谐振束缚的质子通过δ势相互作用，证明了极限吸收原理，建立了定态散射理论，并推导了玻恩近似下的费米散射截面公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究费米1936年提出的经典模型，该模型描述中子与谐振束缚质子通过δ势相互作用的双粒子系统。动机在于对这一历史重要模型进行严格的数学分析，建立完整的散射理论框架。

Method: 采用模型哈密顿量方法，其中中子与谐振束缚质子通过δ势相互作用。首先证明极限吸收原理，然后建立定态散射理论，最后在玻恩近似下推导散射截面公式。

Result: 成功证明了该哈密顿量的极限吸收原理，建立了完整的定态散射理论框架，并推导出了费米散射截面公式，该公式在玻恩近似下成立。

Conclusion: 对费米1936年提出的中子-质子δ势相互作用模型进行了严格的数学分析，建立了完整的散射理论，验证了费米原始公式在玻恩近似下的正确性，为这一经典模型提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a model Hamiltonian, introduced by Fermi in 1936, describing a two-particle system made of a neutron and a harmonically bound proton, where the neutron-proton interaction has the form of a $δ$-potential. For such Hamiltonian we prove the Limiting Absorption Principle and describe the stationary scattering theory. Finally, we derive Fermi's formula for the scattering cross-section valid in the Born approximation.

</details>


### [151] [Weak-Coupling Limit of the Lattice Nonlinear Schrödinger Integral Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09522)
*Felipe Taha Sant'Ana*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子晶格非线性薛定谔模型（等价于自旋s=-1的各向同性Heisenberg XXX自旋链）基态积分方程在弱耦合极限下的行为，发现晶格方程具有双重奇异性，发展了三区域匹配渐近展开，揭示了与Bose-Einstein分布的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子晶格非线性薛定谔模型在弱耦合极限下的基态行为，特别是与连续Lieb-Liniger方程相比，晶格方程具有双重奇异性（驱动项和积分核都退化为δ函数），需要发展新的数学方法来处理这种奇异极限。

Method: 采用三区域匹配渐近展开方法：内区、外区和边界层。通过傅里叶变换建立内区解与Bose-Einstein分布的精确对应，利用Love积分方程的精确对偶性获得总密度展开，通过Wiener-Hopf分解分析边界层行为。

Result: 1. 重标度内区解的傅里叶变换精确等于Bose-Einstein分布；2. 峰值密度以对数方式发散，常数C通过两种独立解析方法和数值计算确定；3. 与圆形电容器Love积分方程的精确对偶给出了总密度展开；4. 通过内区能量恒等式获得每格点基态能量；5. 边界层分析揭示了瞬子作用和复苏跨级数结构。

Conclusion: 量子晶格非线性薛定谔模型在弱耦合极限下展现出丰富的数学结构，包括与统计物理的深刻联系、精确可解性特征以及跨级数结构，为理解晶格量子系统的奇异极限行为提供了系统的解析框架。

Abstract: We study the ground-state integral equation of the quantum lattice nonlinear Schrödinger model -- equivalently the isotropic Heisenberg XXX spin chain with spin $s = -1$ -- in the weak-coupling limit. Unlike the continuous Lieb--Liniger equation, whose driving term is a constant, the lattice equation is doubly singular: both the driving term and the integral kernel degenerate into $δ$-functions as $κ\to 0$. We develop a matched asymptotic expansion with three regions -- inner, outer, and edge. The Fourier transform of the rescaled inner solution is exactly the Bose--Einstein distribution, and the peak density diverges logarithmically with a constant $C$, which we determine analytically via two independent routes and confirm numerically. An exact duality with the Love integral equation for the circular disc capacitor yields the total density expansion. We prove an identity for the inner energy, allowing us to obtain the ground-state energy per site. From the Wiener--Hopf factorisation of the edge boundary layer, we identify the instanton action and predict a resurgent transseries structure.

</details>


### [152] [Pseudo-Riemmanian Lie algebras with coisotropic ideals and integrating the Laplace-Beltrami equation on Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09535)
*A. A. Magazev,I. V. Shirokov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文识别了一类左不变伪黎曼度量，其拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米方程可降为一阶PDE并获得精确解，基于李代数中交换理想的特定正交条件，使用非交换积分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李群上左不变伪黎曼度量，寻找拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米方程可精确求解的特殊类别，探索非交换积分方法的应用潜力。

Method: 基于轨道方法和广义傅里叶变换的非交换积分方法，利用李代数中交换理想的正交条件将拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米方程降为一阶线性PDE，然后显式积分。

Result: 成功识别满足正交条件的度量类别，获得精确解；发现约化方程的对称性通过逆变换产生原始方程的非局部对称算子，这些算子通常是积分-微分型而非多项式型。

Conclusion: 非交换积分方法为伪黎曼几何中的偏微分方程求解提供了有效工具，特别是在经典变量分离法不适用的情况下，能够获得精确解并揭示非局部对称性。

Abstract: We identify a class of left-invariant pseudo-Riemannian metrics on Lie groups for which the Laplace-Beltrami equation reduces to a first-order PDE and admits exact solutions. The defining condition is the existence of a commutative ideal $\mathfrak{h}$ in the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ whose orthogonal complement satisfies $\mathfrak{h}^\perp\subseteq\mathfrak{h}$. Using the noncommutative integration method based on the orbit method and generalized Fourier transforms, we reduce the Laplace--Beltrami equation to a first-order linear PDE, which can then be integrated explicitly. The symmetry of the reduced equation gives rise, via the inverse transform, to nonlocal symmetry operators for the original equation. These operators are generically integro-differential, contrasting with the polynomial symmetries appearing in previously studied classes. The method is illustrated by two examples: the Heisenberg group $\mathrm{H}_3(\mathbb{R})$ with a Lorentzian metric and a four-dimensional non-unimodular group with a metric of signature $(2,2)$. In the latter, classical separation of variables is not directly applicable, yet the noncommutative approach yields explicit solutions and reveals the predicted nonlocal symmetry.

</details>


### [153] [Intertwining Markov Processes via Matrix Product Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09928)
*Rouven Frassek,Jan de Gier,Jimin Li,Frank Verstraete*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种非平衡矩阵乘积算子方法，用于实现一维边界驱动马尔可夫过程的全局对偶变换，并以对称简单排他过程为例展示非平衡边界与满足Liggett条件的平衡边界之间的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部对偶变换与广义对称性相关，但无法处理非平衡边界条件。本文旨在开发一种能够实现全局非平衡对偶变换的框架，揭示看似不同的模型之间的等价关系。

Method: 引入非平衡矩阵乘积算子作为对偶算子，通过广义交换关系交织两个马尔可夫过程，实现全局非平衡对偶。具体构造了对称简单排他过程在非平衡边界条件下的精确对偶算子。

Result: 发现非平衡边界与满足Liggett条件的平衡边界之间存在对偶关系，这意味着Gibbs-Boltzmann测度可以通过对偶算子捕捉非平衡物理。通过物理应用验证了这一原理。

Conclusion: 提出的非平衡矩阵乘积算子方法成功实现了全局对偶变换，揭示了非平衡边界与平衡边界之间的深刻联系，为理解非平衡统计物理提供了新视角。

Abstract: Duality transformations reveal unexpected equivalences between seemingly distinct models. We introduce an out-of-equilibrium generalisation of matrix product operators to implement duality transformations in one-dimensional boundary-driven Markov processes on lattices. In contrast to local dualities associated with generalised symmetries, here the duality operator intertwines two Markov processes via generalised exchange relations and realises the out-of-equilibrium duality globally. We construct these operators exactly for the symmetric simple exclusion process with distinct out-of-equilibrium boundaries. In this case, out-of-equilibrium boundaries are dual to equilibrium boundaries satisfying Liggett's condition, implying that the Gibbs-Boltzmann measure captures out-of-equilibrium physics when leveraging the duality operator. We illustrate this principle through physical applications.

</details>


### [154] [A complete classification of 2d symmetry protected states with symmetric entanglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09959)
*Alex Bols,Wojciech De Roeck,Michiel De Wilde,Bruno de O. Carvalho*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对于可由对称纠缠器从直积态制备的2D量子自旋系统对称保护拓扑态，其分类由上同调群H³(G,U(1))完全描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究2D量子自旋系统的对称保护拓扑态分类问题。虽然已有猜想认为这类态由群上同调H³(G,U(1))分类，但该分类的完备性一直未得到证明。作者旨在为特定类别的对称保护拓扑态提供严格的分类完备性证明。

Method: 作者限制研究范围于可由对称纠缠器从直积态制备的对称保护拓扑态。通过分析这类态的性质，利用群上同调理论和拓扑量子场论框架，证明了H³(G,U(1))分类对该子类的完备性。

Result: 证明了对于可由对称纠缠器从直积态制备的2D量子自旋系统对称保护拓扑态，H³(G,U(1))分类是完备的。这为更广泛的分类猜想提供了重要支持证据，并建立了特定条件下分类的严格数学基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为对称保护拓扑态的分类理论提供了关键进展，证明了在限定条件下H³(G,U(1))分类的完备性，为理解更一般的分类问题奠定了基础，并可能推动拓扑量子物质分类理论的进一步发展。

Abstract: We consider symmetry protected topological states of 2d quantum spin systems, with a finite symmetry group $G$. It has been conjectured that such states are classified by the cohomology group $H^3(G,U(1))$, but the completeness of this classfication is an open problem. We restrict ourselves to symmetry protected topological states that can be prepared from a product state by a symmetric entangler. For this class of states, we prove that the classification by $H^3(G,U(1))$ is complete.

</details>
